## 寻找战争历史展厅的地图资料 · p05-a-01

主题：文娱与运动；情景：询问博物馆展览位置（museum-history-room）。

沟通目标：向博物馆工作人员询问地方历史展览与资料。

背景：游客在虚构地方博物馆询问展览位置，希望了解地图上的边界变化。

人物：Harriet、Museum guide。

起草：author-phase05-a；状态：审阅接受。

- p05-a-01-s01 · Harriet: Where can I find the exhibition about the war?
- p05-a-01-s02 · Museum guide: Upstairs, in the town history room. The map at the entrance is a useful place to start.
- p05-a-01-s03 · Harriet: Does it show the border before the fighting began?
- p05-a-01-s04 · Museum guide: Yes. There is a second map beside it showing the border afterward.
- p05-a-01-s05 · Harriet: And where is the display about the battle near the river?
- p05-a-01-s06 · Museum guide: At the back of that room, beside the display of a bomb that was made safe for the museum.
- p05-a-01-s07 · Harriet: Can I take a leaflet? I would like to compare the two maps properly.
- p05-a-01-s08 · Museum guide: Of course. Here is the exhibition guide, with both maps on page six.
- p05-a-01-s09 · Harriet: Thank you. I will start upstairs with the maps.

目标用法：

- war · n. · 战争；义项 sense-staged-ox-c00444895d67c95c-p05-a-01；来源 ox-c00444895d67c95c；证据 p05-a-01-s01；词形 war。
- battle · n. · 战役；战斗；义项 sense-staged-ox-0de14807f952b8f3-p05-a-01；来源 ox-0de14807f952b8f3；证据 p05-a-01-s05；词形 battle。
- bomb · n. · 炸弹；义项 sense-staged-ox-16e5eb429277e409-p05-a-01；来源 ox-16e5eb429277e409；证据 p05-a-01-s06；词形 bomb。
- border · n. · 边界；义项 sense-staged-ox-c8ce4e8c275c2c3c-p05-a-01；来源 ox-c8ce4e8c275c2c3c；证据 p05-a-01-s03；词形 border。

审阅者：reviewer-phase05-a；整篇结论：accepted。

Harriet requests the war exhibition, narrows the enquiry to before-and-after boundary maps and a river battle, and leaves with a leaflet and a clear upstairs destination. Guide and visitor roles remain consistent; the Chinese title, museum scenario and information-seeking outcome match. The connected exhibits make the military nouns purposeful rather than a vocabulary list.

绑定文件 SHA-256：d0692c6c837b58392ac426e69affeb198594d2559f7cd5308f217f652acf9148

- ox-c00444895d67c95c：accepted；In s01, 'the exhibition about the war' makes war the noun object of about, identifying the armed conflict that the maps and battle display document. This is the selected 战争 sense and the source noun POS.（p05-a-01-s01）。
- ox-0de14807f952b8f3：accepted；In s05, 'the battle near the river' is a definite noun phrase naming one military engagement within the wider war. Asking for its display supports 战役；战斗 rather than the verb use.（p05-a-01-s05）。
- ox-16e5eb429277e409：accepted；In s06, 'a bomb that was made safe' names a countable explosive device displayed in the museum. The article and modifying relative clause establish noun POS; 炸弹 accurately denotes the object.（p05-a-01-s06）。
- ox-c8ce4e8c275c2c3c：accepted；In s03, 'show the border before the fighting began' uses border as the noun object of show. The comparison with the later map establishes a territorial boundary, exactly 边界.（p05-a-01-s03）。

## 分好洗衣与厨房清洁工作 · p05-a-02

主题：居家生活；情景：分配合租房清洁工作（shared-house-cleanup）。

沟通目标：明确待清洁区域和离开房间前的操作

背景：两名室友打算出门前完成洗衣和厨房清洁，先确认分工。

人物：Nadia、Felix。

起草：author-phase05-a；状态：审阅接受。

- p05-a-02-s01 · Nadia: Can you hang out the washing while I clean the kitchen?
- p05-a-02-s02 · Felix: Sure. Is everything in the machine ready to come out?
- p05-a-02-s03 · Nadia: Yes, but hang the red towel on its own. It is still dripping.
- p05-a-02-s04 · Felix: All right. Do you need this cloth for the counter?
- p05-a-02-s05 · Nadia: Yes, please. I need to wipe the whole counter. The blue cloth is for the floor.
- p05-a-02-s06 · Felix: The washing is outside now. Shall I leave the machine door open?
- p05-a-02-s07 · Nadia: Please do, and turn off the kitchen light when you leave.
- p05-a-02-s08 · Felix: Done. I will meet you by the front door.

目标用法：

- washing · n. · 待晾晒的洗好衣物；义项 sense-staged-ox-a4389fe8517de452-p05-a-02；来源 ox-a4389fe8517de452；证据 p05-a-02-s01；词形 washing。
- while · conj. · 在……期间；当……时；义项 sense-staged-ox-cd497417f7f1f9b6-p05-a-02；来源 ox-cd497417f7f1f9b6；证据 p05-a-02-s01；词形 while。
- whole · adj. · 完整的；整个的；义项 sense-staged-ox-66589d8388d0e840-p05-a-02；来源 ox-66589d8388d0e840；证据 p05-a-02-s05；词形 whole。
- cloth · n. · 抹布；义项 sense-staged-ox-e1469485bbaf6e3f-p05-a-02；来源 ox-e1469485bbaf6e3f；证据 p05-a-02-s04；词形 cloth。

审阅者：reviewer-phase05-a；整篇结论：accepted。

Nadia and Felix divide laundry and kitchen work, identify the correct wiping cloth and finish with instructions for leaving the machine and kitchen. The domestic details support the cleaning goal and the closing exchange is practical. 'Hang out the washing' is natural British usage; the Chinese title accurately describes division of chores.

绑定文件 SHA-256：2c9e9a58f309e9630ee6e448ad9cad63b6619833b9f1434bdae43ad243267c24

- ox-a4389fe8517de452：accepted；In s01, 'hang out the washing' uses the mass noun for washed clothes, not an ongoing washing action. The machine being ready to empty and the dripping towel support 待晾晒的洗好衣物.（p05-a-02-s01）。
- ox-cd497417f7f1f9b6：accepted；In s01, while introduces the finite clause 'I clean the kitchen' and links it temporally to hanging laundry. It is the conjunction for simultaneous activities, matching 在……期间；当……时.（p05-a-02-s01）。
- ox-66589d8388d0e840：accepted；In s05, 'the whole counter' has whole modifying counter after the article to require cleaning its entire surface. This is adjective POS and the selected 整个的 meaning.（p05-a-02-s05）。
- ox-e1469485bbaf6e3f：accepted；In s04, 'this cloth for the counter' names a piece of fabric used for wiping, with this as determiner. The next turn contrasts a floor cloth, making 抹布 and noun POS clear.（p05-a-02-s04）。

## 海风较大时选择岸上游览 · p05-a-03

主题：出行与住宿；情景：选择海边游览方式（seaside-boat-trip）。

沟通目标：比较海边游览方式并决定是否乘船。

背景：两位游客在码头向船长了解游船状况，并比较搭船和沿岸步行。

人物：Jules、Captain Moss。

起草：author-phase05-a；状态：审阅接受。

- p05-a-03-s01 · Jules: Is the boat trip still going ahead in this wind?
- p05-a-03-s02 · Captain Moss: Yes, but it will be a bumpy ride today.
- p05-a-03-s03 · Jules: I saw a wave come right over the front of that smaller boat.
- p05-a-03-s04 · Captain Moss: Ours is bigger, but you may still get wet. I am the captain, and I would rather you knew that before buying a ticket.
- p05-a-03-s05 · Jules: Could we walk to the lighthouse instead?
- p05-a-03-s06 · Captain Moss: Certainly. Follow the path straight ahead. It takes about twenty minutes.
- p05-a-03-s07 · Jules: That sounds better for us. We will walk today and try the boat another time.
- p05-a-03-s08 · Captain Moss: No problem. The path starts just beyond the ticket office.

目标用法：

- wave · n. · 海浪；义项 sense-staged-ox-9e9a6ac5ba1fcac1-p05-a-03；来源 ox-9e9a6ac5ba1fcac1；证据 p05-a-03-s03；词形 wave。
- wind · n. · 风；义项 sense-staged-ox-e7d2f295d8b466fb-p05-a-03；来源 ox-e7d2f295d8b466fb；证据 p05-a-03-s01；词形 wind。
- captain · n. · 船长；义项 sense-staged-ox-a0d0e7688fc24f3e-p05-a-03；来源 ox-a0d0e7688fc24f3e；证据 p05-a-03-s04；词形 captain。
- ahead · adv. · 在前方；向前；义项 sense-staged-ox-5da8677b17c626de-p05-a-03；来源 ox-5da8677b17c626de；证据 p05-a-03-s06；词形 ahead。

审阅者：reviewer-phase05-a；整篇结论：accepted。

Jules asks about rough conditions, receives a candid warning from the captain and chooses the lighthouse walk instead; the captain supplies the path. The first-person plural can include Jules's non-speaking travel companion and does not disrupt speaker identity. The seaside-choice scenario, Chinese title and completed choice agree; the directional and marine vocabulary is functional.

绑定文件 SHA-256：16a8bb693988ee298350394d3eb4ad04f504a23beefed438961897494b9442db

- ox-9e9a6ac5ba1fcac1：accepted；In s03, 'I saw a wave come right over the front' uses a wave as the object noun phrase in a perception construction. It is a sea wave, not a greeting gesture, matching 海浪.（p05-a-03-s03）。
- ox-e7d2f295d8b466fb：accepted；In s01, 'in this wind' places wind after a demonstrative inside a prepositional phrase. Weather causing a bumpy boat ride makes the noun 风 sense unambiguous.（p05-a-03-s01）。
- ox-a0d0e7688fc24f3e：accepted；In s04, 'I am the captain' uses the noun as the speaker's occupational complement. The boat and passenger advice identify the ship's commander, matching 船长.（p05-a-03-s04）。
- ox-5da8677b17c626de：accepted；In s06, 'Follow the path straight ahead' uses ahead without an object as a directional adverb modified by straight. It gives the onward walking direction, matching 向前.（p05-a-03-s06）。

## 先拆底座再搬柜子 · p05-a-04

主题：居家生活；情景：协作搬动柜子（cabinet-moving-plan）。

沟通目标：核对通道尺寸并协调柜子移动和暂放位置

背景：室友测量旧柜子和门口，商量如何让柜子通过狭窄入口。

人物：Marta、Leon。

起草：author-phase05-a；状态：审阅接受。

- p05-a-04-s01 · Marta: This cabinet is too wide to get through the doorway like this.
- p05-a-04-s02 · Leon: Can we turn it on its side?
- p05-a-04-s03 · Marta: Not with those wooden feet sticking out. Do you know its weight?
- p05-a-04-s04 · Leon: About thirty kilos without the shelves. The base comes off, though.
- p05-a-04-s05 · Marta: Good. Let us take that part off rather than pull the whole cabinet apart.
- p05-a-04-s06 · Leon: I will remove the shelves and the base first. Can you clear the shoes from the doorway?
- p05-a-04-s07 · Marta: Yes. Then we can carry it sideways and put it against the empty wall.
- p05-a-04-s08 · Leon: Agreed. We will move it together once the path is clear.

目标用法：

- weight · n. · 重量；义项 sense-staged-ox-36a06a077174eba4-p05-a-04；来源 ox-36a06a077174eba4；证据 p05-a-04-s03；词形 weight。
- wide · adj. · 宽的；义项 sense-staged-ox-b838707050b04a46-p05-a-04；来源 ox-b838707050b04a46；证据 p05-a-04-s01；词形 wide。
- wooden · adj. · 木制的；义项 sense-staged-ox-2ee55c114dfb85f0-p05-a-04；来源 ox-2ee55c114dfb85f0；证据 p05-a-04-s03；词形 wooden。
- base · n. · 底座；义项 sense-staged-ox-842b1d9cee4e21cf-p05-a-04；来源 ox-842b1d9cee4e21cf；证据 p05-a-04-s04；词形 base。
- apart · adv. · 分开；成零件；义项 sense-staged-ox-e79b19d0c4276c50-p05-a-04；来源 ox-e79b19d0c4276c50；证据 p05-a-04-s05；词形 apart。

审阅者：reviewer-phase05-a；整篇结论：accepted。

The roommates identify a doorway obstruction, discuss cabinet weight and removable parts, then assign clearing and dismantling tasks before carrying it to an empty wall. Their decisions are causally linked and the closing agreement does not pretend the move is already finished. The title and moving-plan goal fit this outcome.

- ox-36a06a077174eba4：accepted；In s03, 'Do you know its weight?' makes weight the noun object of know, qualified by its. The thirty-kilo answer confirms the physical mass/重量 sense.（p05-a-04-s03）。
- ox-b838707050b04a46：accepted；In s01, 'too wide to get through the doorway' uses wide predicatively after is. The too-adjective-to construction expresses excessive width, matching 宽的 and adjective POS.（p05-a-04-s01）。
- ox-2ee55c114dfb85f0：accepted；In s03, 'those wooden feet' has wooden as an attributive material adjective. It describes cabinet feet made of wood, exactly 木制的.（p05-a-04-s03）。

## 请室友移走窗边蜘蛛 · p05-a-05

主题：居家生活；情景：请室友帮忙移走窗边蜘蛛（spider-window-removal）。

沟通目标：辨认窗边蜘蛛并商定不直接用手接触的移走办法

背景：室友发现蜘蛛停在窗框旁，希望不用手直接接触就把它移到室外。

人物：Owen、Rina。

起草：author-phase05-a；状态：审阅接受。

- p05-a-05-s01 · Owen: Sorry to bother you, but could you help me with the spider by the window?
- p05-a-05-s02 · Rina: The one sitting beside that web?
- p05-a-05-s03 · Owen: Yes. I want to open the window, but I do not want to touch it.
- p05-a-05-s04 · Rina: I can put a glass over it and slide a card underneath.
- p05-a-05-s05 · Owen: Thanks. I will open the other window so you can put it outside.
- p05-a-05-s06 · Rina: There, it is on the card now. Keep that window clear for a moment.
- p05-a-05-s07 · Owen: It is outside. Thank you. I can close this window now.

目标用法：

- web · n. · 蜘蛛网；义项 sense-staged-ox-a761743497bfc347-p05-a-05；来源 ox-a761743497bfc347；证据 p05-a-05-s02；词形 web。
- bother · v. · 打扰；麻烦；义项 sense-staged-ox-485cf8594638c9bd-p05-a-05；来源 ox-485cf8594638c9bd；证据 p05-a-05-s01；词形 bother。

审阅者：reviewer-phase05-a；整篇结论：accepted。

Owen asks Rina to remove a specific window-side spider without direct contact; Rina proposes and uses a glass and card, and Owen confirms the spider is outside. The second window explains the opening/closing sequence. The two-person exchange, Chinese title and completed removal align, with no irrelevant topic shift.

绑定文件 SHA-256：79cf34a7f853328edb525fe533719dd5483c8d37c99569411e958f61cbf5df09

- ox-a761743497bfc347：accepted；In s02, 'beside that web' refers to the structure next to the already identified spider. Web is the noun object of beside, with that as determiner; 蜘蛛网 is clearly intended rather than the internet.（p05-a-05-s02）。
- ox-485cf8594638c9bd：accepted；In s01, 'Sorry to bother you' uses the infinitive verb with you as object in a conventional polite request opener. It means disturb or trouble the roommate, matching 打扰；麻烦.（p05-a-05-s01）。

## 选一条不打扰花丛的观察路线 · p05-a-06

主题：自然与环境；情景：选择花园昆虫观察路线（pollinator-garden-path-choice）。

沟通目标：选定不踩踏花丛的观察位置，并约定向园丁反映挡路枝条

背景：朋友在社区花园观察昆虫，商量站在哪里拍照以及如何处理挡路枝条。

人物：Keiko、Ben。

起草：author-phase05-a；状态：审阅接受。

- p05-a-06-s01 · Keiko: These wild flowers seem to attract more insects than the roses do.
- p05-a-06-s02 · Ben: Look, there is a bee on the purple one. Let us stay on the path.
- p05-a-06-s03 · Keiko: I can get a good photo from here. The beauty of this corner is how untidy it looks.
- p05-a-06-s04 · Ben: Yes, although that low branch is blocking half the path.
- p05-a-06-s05 · Keiko: Shall we move it?
- p05-a-06-s06 · Ben: It is still attached to the tree. Let us tell the gardener rather than break it.
- p05-a-06-s07 · Keiko: Good idea. We can use the other path and watch the flowers from the bench.
- p05-a-06-s08 · Ben: I will mention the branch when we pass the garden office.

- wild · adj. · 野生的；义项 sense-staged-ox-b14b8bc83e2dffc5-p05-a-06；来源 ox-b14b8bc83e2dffc5；证据 p05-a-06-s01；词形 wild。
- bee · n. · 蜜蜂；义项 sense-staged-ox-c13012951cbec4b3-p05-a-06；来源 ox-c13012951cbec4b3；证据 p05-a-06-s02；词形 bee。
- branch · n. · 树枝；义项 sense-staged-ox-3fc728a0bc74145c-p05-a-06；来源 ox-3fc728a0bc74145c；证据 p05-a-06-s04；词形 branch。
- attract · v. · 吸引；义项 sense-staged-ox-7b4570f3f7a1f83a-p05-a-06；来源 ox-7b4570f3f7a1f83a；证据 p05-a-06-s01；词形 attract。
- beauty · n. · 美；美感；义项 sense-staged-ox-62ae02a8e9af285a-p05-a-06；来源 ox-62ae02a8e9af285a；证据 p05-a-06-s03；词形 beauty。

审阅者：reviewer-phase05-a；整篇结论：accepted。

The friends choose a photo position without stepping into the flowers, recognize that the obstructing branch is still attached and agree to use another path and report it. This sustains a practical observation-route goal and ends with a definite next action. The Chinese title and nature scenario reflect the conversation rather than claiming tree work was done.

- ox-c13012951cbec4b3：accepted；In s02, 'there is a bee on the purple one' introduces a countable insect on a flower. The existential noun phrase and pollinator setting directly support noun 蜜蜂.（p05-a-06-s02）。

## 挑选婚礼相册里的照片 · p05-a-07

主题：家庭与人际；情景：挑选婚礼相册照片（wedding-photo-album-plan）。

沟通目标：按婚礼流程选出相册照片并约定摄影师提供版面草稿

背景：新婚的朋友与摄影师一起选相册照片，决定保留仪式和庆祝的不同瞬间。

人物：Aisha、Tom。

起草：author-phase05-a；状态：审阅接受。

- p05-a-07-s01 · Aisha: Wow, I had not seen this photo of everyone dancing!
- p05-a-07-s02 · Tom: It is my favourite from your wedding. You look so cheerful.
- p05-a-07-s03 · Aisha: I was. By then I could stop thinking about being the bride and just enjoy the party.
- p05-a-07-s04 · Tom: Would you like it at the front of the album?
- p05-a-07-s05 · Aisha: My one wish is to tell the story of the day in order. Can we start with the ceremony?
- p05-a-07-s06 · Tom: Yes. This picture of you exchanging rings would make a strong opening.
- p05-a-07-s07 · Aisha: Then the family photo, and the dancing at the end. That shows the whole celebration.
- p05-a-07-s08 · Tom: What about the formal picture beside the car?
- p05-a-07-s09 · Aisha: Leave it out. I would rather have the one where my grandmother is laughing.
- p05-a-07-s10 · Tom: Done. I will arrange those four sections and send you the layout tomorrow.

目标用法：

## 向护士说明不适并配合检查 · p05-a-08

主题：健康与照护；情景：配合诊所护士检查（clinic-examination-cooperation）。

沟通目标：说明身体不适并理解检查期间的要求。

背景：虚构诊所里，一位患者已经向前台报告不适，护士正在当面检查；对话仅练习就诊沟通。

人物：Evan、Nurse Park。

起草：author-phase05-a；状态：审阅接受。

- p05-a-08-s01 · Evan: I feel weak, and there is a tight feeling in my chest.
- p05-a-08-s02 · Nurse Park: I am going to stay with you while the doctor comes over. Has your breathing changed?
- p05-a-08-s03 · Evan: Yes. I feel as if I cannot take a full breath.
- p05-a-08-s04 · Nurse Park: Thank you for telling me. Did that start today?
- p05-a-08-s05 · Evan: About half an hour ago. I am worried because it has not stopped.
- p05-a-08-s06 · Nurse Park: I understand. I need to check your oxygen level with this small device on your finger.
- p05-a-08-s07 · Evan: Do I have to breathe in a special way?
- p05-a-08-s08 · Nurse Park: No, just breathe normally while I take the reading. The doctor is on the way.
- p05-a-08-s09 · Evan: All right. I feel a little more calm knowing someone is here.

Evan reports weakness, chest tightness and changed breathing to an attending nurse, answers an onset question and cooperates with an oxygen reading while the doctor is coming. The nurse's presence and repeated escalation support a plausible care conversation without diagnosis or a false claim of recovery. The final calmer feeling is emotional, and the Chinese symptom-reporting/checking goal is met.

## 转行前先了解入门课程 · p05-a-11

- p05-a-11-s06 · Adrian: Read the beginner course outline, and talk to someone who does the job before you pay for anything.
- p05-a-11-s07 · Sonia: My cousin works in that field. I will ask her about a normal working day and look at the beginner course tonight.
- p05-a-11-s08 · Adrian: Good. Then you can decide whether the subject actually interests you.

## 选择课程期间的住宿并核对抵达安排 · p05-a-12

主题：出行与住宿；情景：核对短期课程住宿并预订（course-stay-price-and-arrival）。

沟通目标：比较住宿总价，核对取消条款和抵达时间后确认预订

背景：学员通过旅行代理了解三晚住宿的总价和取消条件，选择可负担的房间。

人物：Priya、Travel agent。

起草：author-phase05-a；状态：审阅接受。

- p05-a-12-s01 · Priya: I need accommodation for three nights near the language school. I can afford up to three hundred pounds in total.
- p05-a-12-s02 · Travel agent: The guesthouse is two hundred and ten. The hotel next door is three hundred and sixty.
- p05-a-12-s03 · Priya: Does the cheaper room have its own bathroom?
- p05-a-12-s04 · Travel agent: Yes. As your agent, I can send you the full booking agreement before you decide.
- p05-a-12-s05 · Priya: I have it open now. What does this clause about cancellation mean for my booking?
- p05-a-12-s06 · Travel agent: It says you can cancel without a charge until noon two days before arrival. After that, the first night is charged.
- p05-a-12-s07 · Priya: That is clear. My train gets in at six, so I should reach the guesthouse at approximately half past six.

## 把问卷结论限制在受访者范围内 · p05-a-15

主题：研究与论证；情景：讨论调查证据（survey-evidence）。

沟通目标：核对样本、局限与结论的依据

背景：研究小组讨论一份社区设施问卷，识别样本局限并修改结论。

人物：Tara、Joel。

起草：author-phase05-a；状态：审阅接受。

- p05-a-15-s01 · Tara: Can you help me analyse the responses before I send the report?
- p05-a-15-s02 · Joel: Of course. Your opening claim is that most residents support closing the car park.
- p05-a-15-s03 · Tara: That was my belief after reading the comments. Nearly all of them were positive.
- p05-a-15-s04 · Joel: How many people answered, and where did we find them?
- p05-a-15-s05 · Tara: Thirty-two. We handed out the survey at the cycling club.
- p05-a-15-s06 · Joel: Then that is not a sound basis for a conclusion about the whole town.
- p05-a-15-s07 · Tara: You are right. The analysis only tells us about the people who replied.
- p05-a-15-s08 · Joel: Exactly. Their attitude to the plan matters, but other residents may think differently.
- p05-a-15-s09 · Tara: I will change the opening to say that twenty-eight of the thirty-two respondents supported it.
- p05-a-15-s10 · Joel: And explain how we recruited them. Then readers can judge the limits for themselves.
- p05-a-15-s11 · Tara: I will add that beside the result and remove the town-wide claim.

- analyse · v. · 分析；义项 sense-staged-ox-3e3b4750187568ff-p05-a-15；来源 ox-3e3b4750187568ff；证据 p05-a-15-s01；词形 analyse。

- claim · n. · 声称；断言；义项 sense-staged-ox-ff564552592402f6-p05-a-15；来源 ox-ff564552592402f6；证据 p05-a-15-s02；词形 claim。
- belief · n. · 看法；信念；义项 sense-staged-ox-463ecb53a9b1e479-p05-a-15；来源 ox-463ecb53a9b1e479；证据 p05-a-15-s03；词形 belief。
- attitude · n. · 态度；义项 sense-staged-ox-0a1905ad01dd4169-p05-a-15；来源 ox-0a1905ad01dd4169；证据 p05-a-15-s08；词形 attitude。

审阅者：reviewer-phase05-a；整篇结论：accepted。

The researchers challenge a town-wide claim by tracing a 32-person sample to a cycling club, then rewrite the result as 28 of those respondents and disclose recruitment. The limitation and remedy are logically aligned, and the closing commitment matches both the Chinese title and evidence-review goal. The noun/verb analysis pair has distinct syntactic roles rather than artificial repetition.

- p05-a-16-s10 · Quiz host: It was a real achievement. We will credit her result, not turn this into a story about you.
- p05-a-16-s11 · Leo: Understood. I will return the prize voucher now.

## 为夜间电视噪声道歉并关机 · p05-a-17

主题：居家生活；情景：协商夜间电视音量（late-night-television-noise）。

沟通目标：说明噪声影响并商定当天停止观看

背景：一位室友被持续的电视音量打扰，另一位同意今晚停止观看。

人物：Nell、Patrick。

起草：author-phase05-a；状态：审阅接受。

- p05-a-17-s01 · Nell: Could we talk about the television? I am getting annoyed because I can hear every word in my room.
- p05-a-17-s02 · Patrick: Sorry. I did not mean to annoy you. I thought the volume was low enough.
- p05-a-17-s03 · Nell: The dialogue is not too bad, but the music gets much louder. That is the annoying part.
- p05-a-17-s04 · Patrick: I understand. I apologize for keeping you awake.
- p05-a-17-s05 · Nell: Would you mind switching it off for tonight? I have an early start.
- p05-a-17-s06 · Patrick: Absolutely. I will finish the programme tomorrow.
- p05-a-17-s07 · Nell: I appreciate that. Thank you.
- p05-a-17-s08 · Patrick: It is off now. Good night.

目标用法：

- annoy · v. · 使烦恼；打扰；义项 sense-staged-ox-3ea32e5ce7d47262-p05-a-17；来源 ox-3ea32e5ce7d47262；证据 p05-a-17-s02；词形 annoy。
- annoyed · adj. · 恼火的；烦恼的；义项 sense-staged-ox-debc120f0e3e55a6-p05-a-17；来源 ox-debc120f0e3e55a6；证据 p05-a-17-s01；词形 annoyed。
- annoying · adj. · 令人烦恼的；义项 sense-staged-ox-373f2b4788b0ce0b-p05-a-17；来源 ox-373f2b4788b0ce0b；证据 p05-a-17-s03；词形 annoying。
- apologize · v. · 道歉；义项 sense-staged-ox-2e04231208f71044-p05-a-17；来源 ox-2e04231208f71044；证据 p05-a-17-s04；词形 apologize。
- appreciate · v. · 感激；义项 sense-staged-ox-b6fc77fe9a75edf2-p05-a-17；来源 ox-b6fc77fe9a75edf2；证据 p05-a-17-s07；词形 appreciate。
- absolutely · adv. · 当然；完全同意；义项 sense-staged-ox-5d67aa9e4fd96493-p05-a-17；来源 ox-5d67aa9e4fd96493；证据 p05-a-17-s06；词形 absolutely。

审阅者：reviewer-phase05-a；整篇结论：accepted。

Nell identifies why changing TV levels disturb her, Patrick apologizes, and they agree to stop watching tonight; the final line explicitly confirms the television is off. The request, response and resolution match the Chinese title and shared-home goal. The annoyed/annoying contrast emerges naturally from the person's feeling versus the source of irritation; apologize with programme is valid British Oxford-style spelling.

- ox-debc120f0e3e55a6：accepted；In s01, 'I am getting annoyed' uses annoyed as a state adjective after getting. Nell's reaction to audible TV dialogue supports 恼火的；烦恼的.（p05-a-17-s01）。
- ox-373f2b4788b0ce0b：accepted；In s03, 'the annoying part' has annoying modifying part to describe what causes the irritation. The music's volume changes establish 令人烦恼的, distinct from annoyed.（p05-a-17-s03）。

## 请舞蹈教练单独示范转向 · p05-a-18

主题：文娱与运动；情景：澄清初学舞蹈动作并获得反馈（beginner-dance-step-practice）。

沟通目标：弄清第一组舞步的顺序并请教练单独示范转向

背景：初学者练习一组舞步时混淆后退与转身，向教练请求慢速示范。

- p05-a-18-s01 · Paula: Do I step backwards before the turn or after it?

- p05-a-18-s03 · Paula: I keep falling to one side. How do I balance on that foot?
- p05-a-18-s04 · Dance teacher: Do not lock your knee. Bend it a little and make the step smaller.
- p05-a-18-s05 · Paula: That feels steadier, but I still lose track of which way to turn when the music starts.
- p05-a-18-s06 · Dance teacher: Shall I show you the turn on its own, without the other steps?
- p05-a-18-s07 · Paula: Yes, please. I will watch once and then copy you.
- p05-a-18-s08 · Dance teacher: All right. Face the mirror with me, and we will take it slowly.

审阅者：reviewer-phase05-a；整篇结论：accepted。

Reread the complete content_version 2 dialogue and all three target mappings. Revised s05 'lose track of which way to turn' naturally explains Paula's remaining difficulty once music starts. The teacher answers the sequencing question, suggests a steadier step and agrees to demonstrate the isolated turn slowly. The Chinese title and goal promise this bounded instructional outcome, not completed mastery; roles and ending remain consistent, and the earlier wording defect is resolved.

- ox-97ac7650274c92fd：accepted；In s03, 'How do I balance on that foot?' uses balance as a base verb after do and takes an on-phrase. Keeping the body steady on one foot matches 保持平衡.（p05-a-18-s03）。

## 调整合影位置后拍照 · p05-a-19

主题：文娱与运动；情景：调整合影站位（group-portrait-position-adjustment）。

沟通目标：让前排人物后退以露出其他人，并完成合影拍摄

背景：摄影师让站在最前面的人稍向后移，使朋友们都能入镜。

人物：Photographer、Jonah。

起草：author-phase05-a；状态：审阅接受。

- p05-a-19-s01 · Photographer: Jonah, could you move backward a little? You are hiding the person beside you.
- p05-a-19-s02 · Jonah: Like this, or farther back?
- p05-a-19-s03 · Photographer: That is enough. Now turn slightly toward the others.
- p05-a-19-s04 · Jonah: Can you see everyone now?
- p05-a-19-s05 · Photographer: Yes. Hold that position and look at the camera.
- p05-a-19-s06 · Jonah: Ready.
- p05-a-19-s07 · Photographer: Got it. Everyone is in the picture this time.

目标用法：

- backward · adv. · 向后；义项 sense-staged-ox-a5f31922c8c6f400-p05-a-19；来源 ox-a5f31922c8c6f400；证据 p05-a-19-s01；词形 backward。

审阅者：reviewer-phase05-a；整篇结论：accepted。

The photographer asks Jonah to move back, checks the resulting position, adds a small turn and takes the photograph only after everyone is visible. The short sequence has a concrete completed result matching the Chinese title and positioning goal. Backward and toward fit a consistent ordinary American directional style.

## 试吃后决定义卖蛋糕品种 · p05-a-20

主题：饮食沟通；情景：试吃并选择义卖蛋糕（fundraiser-cake-tasting-choice）。

沟通目标：试吃橙味蛋糕后决定将其加入义卖，并确认烘焙数量与装盒分工

起草：author-phase05-a；状态：审阅接受。

- p05-a-20-s01 · Iris: Before I bake another batch, would you try this orange cake?

- p05-a-20-s03 · Iris: I used less than last time. What do you think?
- p05-a-20-s04 · Gavin: I am amazed at how much orange I can taste. It does not need any more sugar.
- p05-a-20-s05 · Iris: Would it be a useful addition to the cake stall, or do we already have too much?
- p05-a-20-s06 · Gavin: We have plenty of chocolate cake, so this would give people a choice.
- p05-a-20-s07 · Iris: Then I will make two orange cakes. Could you bring the boxes?
- p05-a-20-s08 · Gavin: Yes. I will bring four, so there is room for the cakes without stacking them.

- addition · n. · 增加的事物；义项 sense-staged-ox-e0a6d5beb28ab1f1-p05-a-20；来源 ox-e0a6d5beb28ab1f1；证据 p05-a-20-s05；词形 addition。

Iris requests a taste test before further baking, Gavin comments on orange flavour and sugar, and they choose two orange cakes to complement the chocolate selection and arrange boxes. The title and fundraising-stall goal match this product-and-quantity decision. Their roles remain consistent, and the final box offer supports preparation without claiming the new batch is already baked.

- ox-83843706c59fd33e：accepted；In s02, 'One bite should be enough' uses bite as a singular count noun quantified by one. It denotes the small mouthful taken for tasting, matching 一口食物；咬的一口.（p05-a-20-s02）。

## 明确肥皂泡观察任务 · p05-a-22

主题：学习与技能；情景：澄清肥皂泡观察任务（classroom-soap-bubble-observation）。

沟通目标：确认只使用教师准备的材料，并商定记录肥皂泡持续时间的方法

起草：author-phase05-a；状态：审阅接受。

- p05-a-22-s01 · Ruth: Is this clear liquid just water?

- p05-a-22-s03 · Ruth: What are we supposed to measure?
- p05-a-22-s04 · Science teacher: The basic task is to time how long one bubble lasts before it bursts.
- p05-a-22-s05 · Ruth: So I start the timer as soon as the bubble forms?
- p05-a-22-s06 · Science teacher: Yes. Your partner can watch the bubble while you watch the timer.
- p05-a-22-s07 · Ruth: All right. We will record three attempts separately rather than guess an average.
- p05-a-22-s08 · Science teacher: Good. Use the tray I have prepared, and call me if you need more liquid.

Ruth clarifies the liquid and the observation task, the teacher specifies timing a bubble and divides observation duties, and they agree to record three attempts using the prepared tray. The Chinese title and materials/method goal are accurately bounded. The ending asks for more prepared liquid if needed rather than adding a distracting chemical procedure.

## 预约查看顶棚报警器的间歇响声 · p05-a-24

主题：居家生活；情景：预约检查顶棚报警器（ceiling-alarm-inspection-request）。

沟通目标：描述报警器的间歇响声，提供设备编号并约定上门检查时间

背景：住户向楼管描述规律的短促鸣声；楼管确认需要当面查看，尚未声称完成维修。

人物：Amal、Building caretaker。

起草：author-phase05-a；状态：审阅接受。

- p05-a-24-s01 · Amal: Something in my hallway makes a short noise every minute.
- p05-a-24-s02 · Building caretaker: Is it the bell beside your front door or the alarm on the ceiling?
- p05-a-24-s03 · Amal: The alarm. The sound is coming from above me, not from beside the door.
- p05-a-24-s04 · Building caretaker: Does the label on it have a number I can check?
- p05-a-24-s05 · Amal: Yes, it says H seventeen. I can read it from the floor.
- p05-a-24-s06 · Building caretaker: Thank you. I will come at four today to inspect it. Please leave it in place.
- p05-a-24-s07 · Amal: I will be home. Can you call when you reach the main entrance?
- p05-a-24-s08 · Building caretaker: Yes. I have your number, and I will check the unit when I arrive.

- alarm · n. · 报警器；义项 sense-staged-ox-4b2e6098aae34c12-p05-a-24；来源 ox-4b2e6098aae34c12；证据 p05-a-24-s02；词形 alarm。
- bell · n. · 门铃；义项 sense-staged-ox-fec72f00bb94cd97-p05-a-24；来源 ox-fec72f00bb94cd97；证据 p05-a-24-s02；词形 bell。
- ceiling · n. · 天花板；义项 sense-staged-ox-882bbe04d0d662c5-p05-a-24；来源 ox-882bbe04d0d662c5；证据 p05-a-24-s02；词形 ceiling。

审阅者：reviewer-phase05-a；整篇结论：accepted。

Amal distinguishes the ceiling alarm from the doorbell, reads its unit number from the floor and confirms availability for a four-o'clock inspection and entrance call. The caretaker requests that the device remain installed and promises inspection, not an unsupported completed repair. Chinese title, description and scheduling goal accurately reflect that limited outcome.

## 把回忆写作缩小到一次真实经历 · p05-a-25

主题：学习与技能；情景：缩小叙事作业的题材（story-assignment-topic-choice）。

沟通目标：选定真实经历中的具体事件并拟定题目

背景：学生准备一篇回忆录章节作业，教师帮助其从整段童年中选择一个具体事件和标题。

人物：Hugo、Ms Bell。

起草：author-phase05-a；状态：审阅接受。

- p05-a-25-s01 · Hugo: I have written a chapter about every school I went to, but it is already far too long.
- p05-a-25-s02 · Ms Bell: The aim of this assignment is to describe one real event in detail. Which moment do you remember most clearly?
- p05-a-25-s03 · Hugo: The morning I got locked out of school and the caretaker helped me.
- p05-a-25-s04 · Ms Bell: Good. Make that your central event. What happened first?
- p05-a-25-s05 · Hugo: I arrived late, found the gate locked, and thought everyone had gone on a trip.
- p05-a-25-s06 · Ms Bell: That gives the reader a clear problem. You can leave out the history of the other schools.
- p05-a-25-s07 · Hugo: Then I will call it The Morning at the Locked Gate and start with me standing outside.
- p05-a-25-s08 · Ms Bell: That is specific enough. Draft that scene first, and we can look at the ending tomorrow.

- assignment · n. · 作业；义项 sense-staged-ox-1c6f0ba50a2bf0f6-p05-a-25；来源 ox-1c6f0ba50a2bf0f6；证据 p05-a-25-s02；词形 assignment。

- chapter · n. · 书中的章；义项 sense-staged-ox-88b8bd493b515b86-p05-a-25；来源 ox-88b8bd493b515b86；证据 p05-a-25-s01；词形 chapter。

## 澄清悬疑剧线索后继续排练 · p05-a-26

主题：文娱与运动；情景：排练悬疑剧片段（mystery-scene-rehearsal）。

沟通目标：澄清虚构案件的角色关系并确定继续排练的位置。

人物：Ada、Martin。

起草：author-phase05-a；状态：审阅接受。

- p05-a-26-s01 · Ada: Why does my detective ask about the office key here? Has the arrest already happened?
- p05-a-26-s02 · Martin: Not yet. The key is the clue that makes her question the assistant's story.
- p05-a-26-s03 · Ada: So she is suspicious, but she has not proved anything.
- p05-a-26-s04 · Martin: Exactly. The assistant says he has never been inside, then recognizes the key.
- p05-a-26-s05 · Ada: I was playing it as if she already knew he was guilty. I should sound curious instead.
- p05-a-26-s06 · Martin: Yes. Save the stronger reaction for the next scene.
- p05-a-26-s07 · Ada: Let us start again from your line about the locked office.
- p05-a-26-s08 · Martin: Ready. I will give you a moment to look at the key before I answer.

- arrest · n. · 逮捕；义项 sense-staged-ox-a9aeb157b25d2056-p05-a-26；来源 ox-a9aeb157b25d2056；证据 p05-a-26-s01；词形 arrest。

审阅者：reviewer-phase05-a；整篇结论：accepted。

Ada and Martin clarify when the arrest happens, why recognizing the key creates suspicion and how that should change the detective's performance, then choose a precise restart cue. Their actor roles and fictional-case context stay clear. The Chinese title and rehearsal goal match the agreed continuation, without asserting guilt from the clue as a real-world conclusion.

- p05-a-29-s06 · Curator Ellis: Use those descriptions on the labels. Do not add a date unless it is on the card.
- p05-a-29-s07 · June: Neither card gives a date. I will label the material and the recorded location only.

## 先向同伴练习课堂发言开场 · p05-a-32

主题：学习与技能；情景：约定先练习课堂展示的开场（student-presentation-support-plan）。

沟通目标：说明公开发言顾虑，并同意借助笔记向同伴试讲开场

背景：学生担心课堂展示时忘词，同伴帮助其把第一步缩小到私下试讲。

人物：Elsa、Noah。

起草：author-phase05-a；状态：审阅接受。

- p05-a-32-s01 · Elsa: I worry that I will forget everything as soon as I stand up.
- p05-a-32-s02 · Noah: Would it help to practise just the first two sentences with me?
- p05-a-32-s03 · Elsa: Maybe. Everyone else seems so brave about speaking in front of the class.
- p05-a-32-s04 · Noah: They may be nervous too. You do not have to do the whole talk right now.
- p05-a-32-s05 · Elsa: Can I keep my notes in front of me for the first try?
- p05-a-32-s06 · Noah: Of course. I will listen, and then you can tell me which part felt difficult.
- p05-a-32-s07 · Elsa: All right. Let us start with the opening before anyone else arrives.

目标用法：

- worry · v. · 担心；义项 sense-staged-ox-f9ea58e791fc6800-p05-a-32；来源 ox-f9ea58e791fc6800；证据 p05-a-32-s01；词形 worry。

审阅者：reviewer-phase05-a；整篇结论：accepted。

Elsa explains her fear of forgetting, Noah proposes practising only two opening sentences, and she agrees to start privately with notes. The supportive response does not promise that anxiety will disappear. The Chinese title and small-step rehearsal goal match the agreement, and the dialogue closes at a realistic point to begin practice.

- p05-a-34-s06 · Bao: Yes. It is ten minutes along the river, and we can leave whenever we like.

Celia compares the observation tower's hour-long queue with a nearby market, weighs the two-hour time limit and prefers the market's atmosphere. Bao provides a ten-minute route and they choose it. The Chinese title and time/preference-based itinerary goal match the conclusion, with no premature claim of arrival.

## 把烘焙店广告的地址留到结尾 · p05-b-02

- p05-b-02-s04 · Cleo: We could keep three cakes and remove the menu.
- p05-b-02-s05 · Ravi: Yes. Could we conclude with the shop name and address instead of the slogan?
- p05-b-02-s06 · Cleo: That gives viewers four seconds to read it. Shall I keep the opening music?
- p05-b-02-s07 · Ravi: Please do. Make those changes and send me the shorter version.
- p05-b-02-s08 · Cleo: I will. The address will stay on screen until the music ends.

Ravi raises a genuine legibility problem in the bakery advertisement; Cleo trades excessive cake/menu shots for four seconds of address display. ## 为漏水的水壶安排换货 · p05-b-05

主题：消费与服务；情景：协商故障水壶换货（faulty-kettle-exchange）。

沟通目标：说明水壶漏水问题并确认换货所需物品和到店安排。

背景：顾客联系家电店，要求处理刚买来便漏水的水壶。

人物：Marta、Ben。

起草：author-phase05-b；状态：审阅接受。

- p05-b-05-s01 · Marta: I have a complaint about the kettle I bought yesterday. Water leaks from the base.
- p05-b-05-s02 · Ben: I am sorry. Have you stopped using it?
- p05-b-05-s03 · Marta: Yes. I am disappointed because I bought it after reading such good reviews.
- p05-b-05-s04 · Ben: That is a disappointing start. We can inspect it at the counter and replace it if it is faulty.
- p05-b-05-s05 · Marta: As a consumer, I expected it to last more than a day. Can you arrange a replacement at the service counter?
- p05-b-05-s06 · Ben: Yes. Bring the kettle and receipt to the service counter. A replacement of the same model is in stock.
- p05-b-05-s07 · Marta: I would like to exchange it this afternoon, then.
- p05-b-05-s08 · Ben: I will leave a note for the counter team. They will check the leak with you when you arrive.

- complaint · n. · 投诉；义项 sense-staged-ox-d109497183210ebc-p05-b-05；来源 ox-d109497183210ebc；证据 p05-b-05-s01；词形 complaint。

- disappointing · adj. · 令人失望的；义项 sense-staged-ox-6d09b67cff562371-p05-b-05；来源 ox-6d09b67cff562371；证据 p05-b-05-s04；词形 disappointing。
- exchange · v. · 换货；更换；义项 sense-staged-ox-71595d21b4b9e4bd-p05-b-05；来源 ox-71595d21b4b9e4bd；证据 p05-b-05-s07；词形 exchange。

审阅者：reviewer-phase05-b；整篇结论：accepted。

Marta explains a newly leaking kettle and disappointment; Ben checks that she has stopped using it, then arranges inspection and a conditional same-model exchange with receipt. The afternoon visit and counter note close the service plan without falsely claiming the exchange is already completed. 'As a consumer' is somewhat formal but credible in a complaint.

## 先把复杂题目拆成三步 · p05-b-06

主题：学习与技能；情景：制定小组学习任务目标（study-task-target-plan）。

沟通目标：把宽泛的学习目标分成可执行任务并约定先试一种策略

人物：Sana、Joel。

- p05-b-06-s01 · Sana: This problem is so complex that I do not know where to begin.
- p05-b-06-s02 · Joel: Which part is giving you difficulty?
- p05-b-06-s03 · Sana: I am confused about the two diagrams. I keep treating them as the same shape.
- p05-b-06-s04 · Joel: The similar labels can confuse anyone. Let us redraw them on separate pages.
- p05-b-06-s05 · Sana: Then I can concentrate on one shape at a time.
- p05-b-06-s06 · Joel: Yes. To use our time effectively, we should identify the shapes, list the measurements, and only then calculate.
- p05-b-06-s07 · Sana: Let us try that order on this problem before doing the others.
- p05-b-06-s08 · Joel: All right. You draw the first shape, and I will check its labels with you.

- complex · adj. · 复杂的；义项 sense-staged-ox-a8030036f1386514-p05-b-06；来源 ox-a8030036f1386514；证据 p05-b-06-s01；词形 complex。

- effectively · adv. · 有效地；义项 sense-staged-ox-eb4c1e8d6a133332-p05-b-06；来源 ox-eb4c1e8d6a133332；证据 p05-b-06-s06；词形 effectively。

审阅者：reviewer-phase05-b；整篇结论：accepted。

Sana's difficulty distinguishing two geometry diagrams motivates Joel's redraw-and-sequence strategy: identify shapes, list measurements, calculate. ## 先核对印刷样稿再确认交付 · p05-b-08

主题：工作与协作；情景：协调项目交付（project-delivery）。

沟通目标：澄清依赖、范围、风险与承诺

背景：设计负责人和印刷协调员讨论手册交付前的文件检查与时间风险。

人物：Priya、Leon。

起草：author-phase05-b；状态：审阅接受。

- p05-b-08-s01 · Priya: The brochures are due on Friday. Is that still your expected delivery date?
- p05-b-08-s02 · Leon: It is, provided we approve the proof today. The current file has an old telephone number.
- p05-b-08-s03 · Priya: I am currently correcting that. Can we determine whether the printer has started the job?
- p05-b-08-s04 · Leon: They have not. They are waiting for our approval.
- p05-b-08-s05 · Priya: Good. What would be the consequence of approving it tomorrow instead?
- p05-b-08-s06 · Leon: They could not promise Friday. The delivery might move to Monday.
- p05-b-08-s07 · Priya: Then I will send the corrected file by two. Please check the number before approving it.
- p05-b-08-s08 · Leon: I will. Once it is approved, they can deliver the boxes to reception on Friday morning.
- p05-b-08-s09 · Priya: I will tell reception to expect them, but only after you confirm the printer has accepted the schedule.
- p05-b-08-s10 · Leon: Agreed. I will check the proof and get their confirmation this afternoon.

Priya owns the correction and reception notice; Leon owns checking the proof and obtaining the printer's confirmation. Their conditional commitments avoid an unsupported delivery guarantee and match the project-risk goal and Chinese title.

## 为小书房选取暖器 · p05-b-11

主题：消费与服务；情景：比较电暖器标签（heater-label-comparison）。

沟通目标：核对供电要求和功能后选定适合房间的型号

背景：顾客通过铭牌和产品说明比较两款电暖器。

人物：Felix、Asha。

起草：author-phase05-b；状态：审阅接受。

- p05-b-11-s01 · Felix: Do these heaters consume the same amount of electricity?
- p05-b-11-s02 · Asha: At full power, this one draws one kilowatt and that one draws two. Both run from a standard wall socket.
- p05-b-11-s03 · Felix: My study is tiny. Would the smaller one be effective there?
- p05-b-11-s04 · Asha: Its label says it is designed for rooms of that size. It also has a timer.
- p05-b-11-s05 · Felix: What is its main disadvantage?
- p05-b-11-s06 · Asha: It takes longer to warm a larger room, but you said you only need it for the study.
- p05-b-11-s07 · Felix: That is right. I will take the smaller model with the timer.
- p05-b-11-s08 · Asha: Here is the manual. It includes the clearance distances you need to check before using it.

目标用法：

- consume · v. · 消耗；义项 sense-staged-ox-a62aa47b6ee27146-p05-b-11；来源 ox-a62aa47b6ee27146；证据 p05-b-11-s01；词形 consume。
- disadvantage · n. · 缺点；不利之处；义项 sense-staged-ox-45a82a75f83c1fc8-p05-b-11；来源 ox-45a82a75f83c1fc8；证据 p05-b-11-s05；词形 disadvantage。
- effective · adj. · 有效的；义项 sense-staged-ox-ef21cd146a23f66a-p05-b-11；来源 ox-ef21cd146a23f66a；证据 p05-b-11-s03；词形 effective。

审阅者：reviewer-phase05-b；整篇结论：accepted。

Felix and Asha compare the heaters' power draw, ordinary socket use, small-room suitability and timer. The larger-room drawback does not contradict choosing the smaller model for a tiny study; the manual/clearance reminder is a natural service ending. Title and goal reflect the actual product choice.

绑定文件 SHA-256：e411e426db452de23e0022861df1e18b897b3bf9ae85ee738fea91d2de4e55ba

- ox-a62aa47b6ee27146：accepted；s01 'consume the same amount of electricity' is a transitive verb for using energy; the full-power comparison in s02 supports 消耗.（p05-b-11-s01）。
- ox-45a82a75f83c1fc8：accepted；s05 'its main disadvantage' is a count noun for an unfavourable feature, answered with slower warming of a larger room.（p05-b-11-s05）。
- ox-ef21cd146a23f66a：accepted；s03 'be effective there' uses a predicative adjective for successfully heating the tiny study; the room-size label responds to precisely that concern.（p05-b-11-s03）。

## 先重练讲座的开场 · p05-b-12

主题：工作与协作；情景：调整社区讲座的讲法（community-talk-rehearsal）。

沟通目标：澄清讲座结构和重点后决定重练开场

背景：社区讲者担心自己的堆肥讲座太难，朋友帮助缩小重点并安排重练。

人物：Gavin、Lou。

起草：author-phase05-b；状态：审阅接受。

- p05-b-12-s01 · Gavin: I am determined to make this talk useful, but I keep losing my place.
- p05-b-12-s02 · Lou: You have put a lot of effort into it. There may simply be too much to cover in ten minutes.
- p05-b-12-s03 · Gavin: I wanted to convince everyone that composting is worth trying.
- p05-b-12-s04 · Lou: Then start with the problem of food waste, show one small bin, and explain the first step.
- p05-b-12-s05 · Gavin: That sounds clearer than explaining all the science. I feel more confident about that version.
- p05-b-12-s06 · Lou: You can encourage people to ask questions at the end instead of answering every possible question in advance.
- p05-b-12-s07 · Gavin: Will you listen while I try the new opening?
- p05-b-12-s08 · Lou: Of course. Start with what goes into the bin, and I will stop you if anything is unclear.

目标用法：

- confident · adj. · 有信心的；义项 sense-staged-ox-f8dbcdded7e8f851-p05-b-12；来源 ox-f8dbcdded7e8f851；证据 p05-b-12-s05；词形 confident。
- encourage · v. · 鼓励；义项 sense-staged-ox-5d908ff27ce83e09-p05-b-12；来源 ox-5d908ff27ce83e09；证据 p05-b-12-s06；词形 encourage。
- effort · n. · 努力；义项 sense-staged-ox-0a11975fb1299682-p05-b-12；来源 ox-0a11975fb1299682；证据 p05-b-12-s02；词形 effort。
- determined · adj. · 下定决心的；义项 sense-staged-ox-f18e87bd2d03bcbd-p05-b-12；来源 ox-f18e87bd2d03bcbd；证据 p05-b-12-s01；词形 determined。
- convince · v. · 使相信；说服；义项 sense-staged-ox-9932a2e4debeee29-p05-b-12；来源 ox-9932a2e4debeee29；证据 p05-b-12-s03；词形 convince。

审阅者：reviewer-phase05-b；整篇结论：accepted。

Gavin's overloaded ten-minute composting talk is reduced to a food-waste problem, a small bin and a first step, reserving questions for the end. Lou offers to hear a new opening, so the rehearsal-planning goal is reached. The closing bin-content cue reasonably focuses the practical portion of that opening; it need not exclude the preceding problem sentence.

- ox-f8dbcdded7e8f851：accepted；s05 'feel more confident about that version' is a predicative adjective for the speaker's increased assurance after simplifying the talk.（p05-b-12-s05）。
- ox-5d908ff27ce83e09：accepted；s06 'encourage people to ask questions' uses the transitive verb plus object and to-infinitive in its natural motivating/prompting construction.（p05-b-12-s06）。
- ox-0a11975fb1299682：accepted；s02 'put a lot of effort into it' is the mass noun in a standard collocation for the work Gavin invested in the talk.（p05-b-12-s02）。
- ox-f18e87bd2d03bcbd：accepted；s01 'I am determined to make this talk useful' is a resolve adjective with a to-infinitive, not the past passive of establishing a fact.（p05-b-12-s01）。

## 选靠近村庄的乡间小屋 · p05-b-13

主题：出行与住宿；情景：选择乡间住宿与探索范围（rural-cottage-route-choice）。

沟通目标：比较两处乡间住宿的位置并决定先询问靠近村庄的小屋。

背景：朋友讨论不租车的周末乡间旅行。

人物：Mila、Tom。

起草：author-phase05-b；状态：审阅接受。

- p05-b-13-s01 · Mila: I would love a weekend in the countryside, but I do not want to rent a car.
- p05-b-13-s02 · Tom: There is a cottage beside the village station. The other one is five miles from the nearest shop.
- p05-b-13-s03 · Mila: Then the village sounds like a better destination. What can we do from there?
- p05-b-13-s04 · Tom: We can explore the old mill and the footpaths on the eastern side of the village.
- p05-b-13-s05 · Mila: Are those paths within walking distance of the cottage?
- p05-b-13-s06 · Tom: Yes. The map shows the first one starting behind the station.
- p05-b-13-s07 · Mila: Let us ask whether the cottage by the station is available. We can plan the walks after that.
- p05-b-13-s08 · Tom: Agreed. I will send the owner an availability request for next weekend.

目标用法：

- cottage · n. · 乡间小屋；义项 sense-staged-ox-0cc79732c70491bb-p05-b-13；来源 ox-0cc79732c70491bb；证据 p05-b-13-s02；词形 cottage。
- countryside · n. · 乡村地区；义项 sense-staged-ox-0c5a0b74285006fc-p05-b-13；来源 ox-0c5a0b74285006fc；证据 p05-b-13-s01；词形 countryside。
- destination · n. · 目的地；义项 sense-staged-ox-18e43ce02a151e2a-p05-b-13；来源 ox-18e43ce02a151e2a；证据 p05-b-13-s03；词形 destination。
- eastern · adj. · 东部的；义项 sense-staged-ox-56a561c1b0a0e488-p05-b-13；来源 ox-56a561c1b0a0e488；证据 p05-b-13-s04；词形 eastern。
- explore · v. · 探索；游览；义项 sense-staged-ox-6c69a3f00d75a125-p05-b-13；来源 ox-6c69a3f00d75a125；证据 p05-b-13-s04；词形 explore。

审阅者：reviewer-phase05-b；整篇结论：accepted。

The no-car constraint makes the cottage by the village station preferable to a remote one. Walkable paths and the mill support that preference, and the pair agree to request availability rather than claim a booking. The title is a selection for inquiry and the Chinese goal states that limited outcome accurately.

## 核对探险出发前的装备 · p05-b-14

主题：文娱与运动；情景：核对带队探险出发安排（guided-expedition-departure-check）。

沟通目标：确认领队经验、出发时间与装备检查后前往集合点。

背景：参加者在游客中心为一项有向导带队的洞穴考察做最后确认。

人物：Aiden、Soraya。

起草：author-phase05-b；状态：审阅接受。

- p05-b-14-s01 · Aiden: Is the expedition still going ahead despite the rain?
- p05-b-14-s02 · Soraya: Yes. The guide has checked the route. If conditions change, she will cancel it.
- p05-b-14-s03 · Aiden: Good. I have never been inside a cave before.
- p05-b-14-s04 · Soraya: She is an experienced guide, and this is the introductory route. What is worrying you?
- p05-b-14-s05 · Aiden: Mostly the equipment. Is a helmet essential?
- p05-b-14-s06 · Soraya: Yes. The guide will fit it and check it before departure at ten.
- p05-b-14-s07 · Aiden: Should I fasten the strap now?
- p05-b-14-s08 · Soraya: Wait until she has adjusted it for you. Take your helmet to the marked meeting point.
- p05-b-14-s09 · Aiden: All right. I will go there now and ask her to check the fit.

- departure · n. · 出发；义项 sense-staged-ox-84c11ef5fa28faad-p05-b-14；来源 ox-84c11ef5fa28faad；证据 p05-b-14-s06；词形 departure。

- expedition · n. · 有组织的考察或探险；义项 sense-staged-ox-1fa71b2dcb962de9-p05-b-14；来源 ox-1fa71b2dcb962de9；证据 p05-b-14-s01；词形 expedition。
- experienced · adj. · 有经验的；义项 sense-staged-ox-663020309088a267-p05-b-14；来源 ox-663020309088a267；证据 p05-b-14-s04；词形 experienced。
- essential · adj. · 必不可少的；义项 sense-staged-ox-a0a07cad210d330b-p05-b-14；来源 ox-a0a07cad210d330b；证据 p05-b-14-s05；词形 essential。
- fasten · v. · 扣紧；系牢；义项 sense-staged-ox-3d46641cff5ac10b-p05-b-14；来源 ox-3d46641cff5ac10b；证据 p05-b-14-s07；词形 fasten。

审阅者：reviewer-phase05-b；整篇结论：accepted。

Aiden's first cave trip prompts weather, guide-experience and helmet questions. Soraya consistently refers fitting and route decisions to the guide, specifies ten o'clock and sends him to the meeting point. The endpoint is going for the fitting rather than claiming it already happened; this matches the departure-check goal.

## 调整演出服装并试走入场 · p05-b-15

主题：文娱与运动；情景：排练家庭演出的服装与入场（family-show-costume-rehearsal）。

沟通目标：调整演出服装长度并约定鼓点提示后试走入场。

背景：社区演出的两位表演者准备为家庭观众排练一段滑稽入场。

人物：Bea、Marcus。

起草：author-phase05-b；状态：审阅接受。

- p05-b-15-s01 · Bea: I am fully dressed, but this costume catches under my shoes.
- p05-b-15-s02 · Marcus: Then we should shorten the trousers before you try the entrance.
- p05-b-15-s03 · Bea: Will there still be a drum playing as I walk in?
- p05-b-15-s04 · Marcus: Yes. I will give you three slow beats, then stop when you reach the chair.
- p05-b-15-s05 · Bea: We want to entertain the children, not have them watch me fall over.
- p05-b-15-s06 · Marcus: Exactly. This is gentle family entertainment. The joke should be the enormous hat, not a real accident.
- p05-b-15-s07 · Bea: I have folded the trousers up. Does that look safer?
- p05-b-15-s08 · Marcus: Yes, your shoes are clear now. Let us walk through it slowly without the drum first.

- costume · n. · 演出服装；义项 sense-staged-ox-12f8afd1def482d8-p05-b-15；来源 ox-12f8afd1def482d8；证据 p05-b-15-s01；词形 costume。

- drum · n. · 鼓；义项 sense-staged-ox-809300d3a87d6936-p05-b-15；来源 ox-809300d3a87d6936；证据 p05-b-15-s03；词形 drum。
- entertain · v. · 使娱乐；逗乐；义项 sense-staged-ox-1741ef8de9079f00-p05-b-15；来源 ox-1741ef8de9079f00；证据 p05-b-15-s05；词形 entertain。
- entertainment · n. · 娱乐活动或表演；义项 sense-staged-ox-f104c7b838509969-p05-b-15；来源 ox-f104c7b838509969；证据 p05-b-15-s06；词形 entertainment。

审阅者：reviewer-phase05-b；整篇结论：accepted。

Bea's trouser length creates a practical rehearsal obstacle; Marcus explains the three-beat cue and chair endpoint, and they first adjust the costume then agree to a slow walk-through without percussion. Performer roles and safety motivation remain coherent; title and goal describe planning and beginning that rehearsal.

## 为家庭观看改选纪录片集数 · p05-b-16

- p05-b-16-s03 · Nadia: I thought it was mainly about how people rebuilt the town.
- p05-b-16-s04 · Hugo: That is the second one. In the first, villagers escape before the enemy arrives.
- p05-b-16-s05 · Nadia: Does it show what happens to the people who stay?
- p05-b-16-s06 · Hugo: Yes. The soldiers are cruel to them, and I found those scenes hard to watch.
- p05-b-16-s07 · Nadia: Then I would rather not choose that one for tonight. Have you seen the second?
- p05-b-16-s08 · Hugo: I have. It follows families repairing their homes, with no battle scenes.
- p05-b-16-s09 · Nadia: Let us watch that one together and leave the first for another time.

## 用音效表现爆炸 · p05-b-17

主题：文娱与运动；情景：为短片改用安全音效（film-sound-effect-substitution）。

沟通目标：明确不用真实爆炸效果并安排通过镜头和录音表现。

背景：学生短片导演与演员核对一段只需暗示事件的镜头。

人物：Rosa、Kit。

起草：author-phase05-b；状态：审阅接受。

- p05-b-17-s01 · Rosa: The script says there is an explosion outside. Are we filming one?
- p05-b-17-s02 · Kit: No. Nothing should actually explode on set. We will add a recorded sound later.
- p05-b-17-s03 · Rosa: What do you need me to do when I hear it?
- p05-b-17-s04 · Kit: You will not hear it during filming. I will say your cue, and you look towards the window.
- p05-b-17-s05 · Rosa: So the camera stays on my reaction, and the event is never shown.
- p05-b-17-s06 · Kit: Exactly. Let us rehearse the look once, without any effects.

目标用法：

- explode · v. · 爆炸；义项 sense-staged-ox-de743952b2d45276-p05-b-17；来源 ox-de743952b2d45276；证据 p05-b-17-s02；词形 explode。

审阅者：reviewer-phase05-b；整篇结论：accepted。

Rosa's question as actor is answered by Kit's direction: record only her reaction and add the off-screen event's sound later. The cue clarification and rehearsal without effects make the scene plan coherent and safe; title and goal accurately describe implying an explosion, not producing one.

## 核对定义并商定图示修改 · p05-b-20

- p05-b-20-s03 · Ewan: Should I define a compound on the same page?
- p05-b-20-s04 · Lina: Yes, otherwise the two pictures may confuse people. Your diagram needs labels.
- p05-b-20-s05 · Ewan: I will label this group as oxygen and the other as water.
- p05-b-20-s06 · Lina: Good. Show that the water contains two types of atom joined together, while the oxygen contains only one type.
- p05-b-20-s07 · Ewan: I will add that explanation below the pictures, then ask our teacher to check the page.
- p05-b-20-s08 · Lina: That will make the difference much easier to see.

主题：社会与公共事务；情景：区分不同层级的选举议题（district-election-question-planning）。

- p05-b-24-s03 · Daria: Then it would not test what they could actually do in the council.
- p05-b-24-s04 · Noah: Exactly. Your question about the closed local playground fits their role better.
- p05-b-24-s05 · Daria: I will ask when they would reopen it and how they would pay for repairs.
- p05-b-24-s06 · Noah: Keep the immigration question on a separate page for the national candidates.
- p05-b-24-s07 · Daria: Good. I now have one set of questions for each level rather than mixing them together.

## 不要把出口增长写成整体经济复苏 · p05-b-25

主题：研究与论证；情景：核对经济报道的结论范围（economic-news-claim-check）。

沟通目标：区分单一行业出口数据和整体经济结论并修订报道标题。

背景：新闻写作课上，两名同学检查一份虚构地方经济报道的草稿。

人物：Theo、Yasmin。

起草：author-phase05-b；状态：审阅接受。

- p05-b-25-s01 · Theo: My headline says the economy has fully recovered. Is that supported by the figures?
- p05-b-25-s02 · Yasmin: The report only says local firms export more bicycles than last year.
- p05-b-25-s03 · Theo: That is still an economic improvement, though.
- p05-b-25-s04 · Yasmin: For those firms, yes. But we have no figures for jobs, household spending, or other industries.
- p05-b-25-s05 · Theo: So the headline makes a much bigger claim than the evidence allows.
- p05-b-25-s06 · Yasmin: Right. Can you say exactly what grew instead?
- p05-b-25-s07 · Theo: I will change it to Local bicycle sales abroad rise, and give the percentage in the first paragraph.
- p05-b-25-s08 · Yasmin: That matches the report. Keep the wider recovery question open until you have more evidence.

- economic · adj. · 经济方面的；义项 sense-staged-ox-277b2e10c718cc92-p05-b-25；来源 ox-277b2e10c718cc92；证据 p05-b-25-s03；词形 economic。

- export · v. · 出口；义项 sense-staged-ox-64004673afae6c22-p05-b-25；来源 ox-64004673afae6c22；证据 p05-b-25-s02；词形 export。

审阅者：reviewer-phase05-b；整篇结论：accepted。

Theo's overbroad recovery headline is challenged because only bicycle exports grew; Yasmin names missing employment, spending and industry evidence. Theo narrows the headline to sales abroad and keeps the wider claim open. The invented class exercise, speaker roles and corrected conclusion match the Chinese title and goal.

- p05-b-26-s06 · Alma: Good. I will record the donation and find someone to help with the table.

- p05-b-33-s05 · Ada: Relief is the strongest emotion. I got through the talk instead of walking away.
- p05-b-33-s06 · Miles: Would it help to try the opening again with just me listening?
- p05-b-33-s07 · Ada: Yes, but I dislike being interrupted every time I hesitate. Please let me finish before giving feedback.
- p05-b-33-s08 · Miles: I can do that. We can practise tomorrow, and I will keep my notes until the end.
- p05-b-33-s09 · Ada: Thank you. That feels like a useful next step, not another test.

背景：丹因为临时忘记约定而让朋友梅等候，现在主动解释。

起草：author-phase05-c；状态：审阅接受。

- p05-c-05-s01 · Dan: I owe you an apology for leaving you waiting yesterday.
- p05-c-05-s02 · Mei: I was mad because you did not answer my messages. I thought something had happened.
- p05-c-05-s03 · Dan: I feel guilty about that. I lost track of time, and that is not a good excuse.
- p05-c-05-s04 · Mei: I would rather you be honest than invent a reason.
- p05-c-05-s05 · Dan: Our friendship matters to me. Could I make it up to you with lunch on Saturday?
- p05-c-05-s06 · Mei: Lunch would be nice. Can we meet at twelve at the usual cafe?
- p05-c-05-s07 · Dan: Yes. I will put it in my calendar now, and let you know early if anything changes.
- p05-c-05-s08 · Mei: Thanks. I am glad we talked about it.

目标用法：

- mad · adj. · 生气的；义项 sense-staged-ox-03808c3f449801d3-p05-c-05；来源 ox-03808c3f449801d3；证据 p05-c-05-s02；词形 mad。
- guilty · adj. · 感到内疚的；义项 sense-staged-ox-3edc677c55a728d8-p05-c-05；来源 ox-3edc677c55a728d8；证据 p05-c-05-s03；词形 guilty。
- honest · adj. · 诚实的；义项 sense-staged-ox-225af292971a6516-p05-c-05；来源 ox-225af292971a6516；证据 p05-c-05-s04；词形 honest。
- friendship · n. · 友谊；义项 sense-staged-ox-9c395623fe0ffa84-p05-c-05；来源 ox-9c395623fe0ffa84；证据 p05-c-05-s05；词形 friendship。
- glad · adj. · 高兴的；欣慰的；义项 sense-staged-ox-a2a0b2b78968ebe8-p05-c-05；来源 ox-a2a0b2b78968ebe8；证据 p05-c-05-s08；词形 glad。

审阅者：reviewer-phase05-c；整篇结论：accepted。

Dan takes responsibility for leaving Mei waiting, Mei explains her anger, and they agree on lunch at twelve on Saturday plus earlier notice of changes. The relieved ending fulfils the apology/reconnection goal and title. American mad and the rather-you-be construction are acceptable here.

绑定文件 SHA-256：62ad995b5e3dc6363756eb950f87818046e6e6e5e9a6769e547b9e8d96ade625

- ox-03808c3f449801d3：accepted；s02: 'I was mad because' is a predicative adjective for anger over unanswered messages, not mental illness; the American angry sense matches 生气的.（p05-c-05-s02）。
- ox-3edc677c55a728d8：accepted；s03: 'feel guilty about that' is an adjective with the normal about complement for remorse over keeping Mei waiting, not a legal verdict.（p05-c-05-s03）。
- ox-225af292971a6516：accepted；s04: 'you be honest' is a predicative adjective contrasted with inventing a reason. It illustrates 诚实的; the American rather construction is acceptable.（p05-c-05-s04）。
- ox-9c395623fe0ffa84：accepted；s05: 'our friendship matters to me' makes the abstract relationship noun the subject. Offering to make amends supports 友谊/source n.（p05-c-05-s05）。
- ox-a2a0b2b78968ebe8：accepted；s08: 'I am glad we talked about it' is a predicative adjective plus content clause expressing Mei's relief; 高兴的/欣慰的 fit the resolved conflict.（p05-c-05-s08）。

## 今晚选一部不吓人的电影 · p05-c-06

主题：文娱与运动；情景：商定朋友电影之夜（friends-film-night）。

沟通目标：兼顾时间与喜好选定影片

背景：朋友安娜和杰克在晚饭后挑选今晚一起看的电影。

人物：Anna、Jack。

起草：author-phase05-c；状态：审阅接受。

- p05-c-06-s01 · Anna: We have time for one film before ten. What about The Old House?
- p05-c-06-s02 · Jack: Is that the one with a ghost in the bathroom?
- p05-c-06-s03 · Anna: Yes. The trailer looks frightening, but people say the ending is funny.
- p05-c-06-s04 · Jack: I do not enjoy horror. I was frightened for hours after the last film you chose.
- p05-c-06-s05 · Anna: Sorry, I do not want to frighten you again. We can choose something else.
- p05-c-06-s06 · Jack: Thanks. I had a horrible day at work, and I would rather laugh tonight.
- p05-c-06-s07 · Anna: How about the comedy with the two lost tourists? It is only ninety minutes.
- p05-c-06-s08 · Jack: That sounds much better. Let us watch that one.

目标用法：

- ghost · n. · 鬼魂；义项 sense-staged-ox-f283d33af73d4a22-p05-c-06；来源 ox-f283d33af73d4a22；证据 p05-c-06-s02；词形 ghost。
- frightening · adj. · 令人害怕的；义项 sense-staged-ox-3883335955d2b6db-p05-c-06；来源 ox-3883335955d2b6db；证据 p05-c-06-s03；词形 frightening。
- horror · n. · 恐怖题材或恐怖类型；义项 sense-staged-ox-2ea345db0e8e144c-p05-c-06；来源 ox-2ea345db0e8e144c；证据 p05-c-06-s04；词形 horror。
- frightened · adj. · 感到害怕的；义项 sense-staged-ox-20d513d2ccfa3145-p05-c-06；来源 ox-20d513d2ccfa3145；证据 p05-c-06-s04；词形 frightened。
- frighten · v. · 使害怕；义项 sense-staged-ox-2b897660b3422458-p05-c-06；来源 ox-2b897660b3422458；证据 p05-c-06-s05；词形 frighten。
- horrible · adj. · 糟糕的；令人不快的；义项 sense-staged-ox-6ae1d9f3d990d74d-p05-c-06；来源 ox-6ae1d9f3d990d74d；证据 p05-c-06-s06；词形 horrible。

审阅者：reviewer-phase05-c；整篇结论：accepted。

Anna proposes a scary film, respects Jack's aversion and difficult day, and offers a ninety-minute comedy that he accepts. Fear vocabulary is motivated by an actual decision. Speaker identities, time consideration, title and preference-based goal align.

- ox-3883335955d2b6db：accepted；s03: 'the trailer looks frightening' is an adjective complement after looks describing fear-inducing quality, correctly contrasting with the person's frightened state.（p05-c-06-s03）。

- ox-20d513d2ccfa3145：accepted；s04: 'I was frightened for hours' describes Jack's continuing emotional state, evidencing the adjective 感到害怕的 rather than an agentive passive action.（p05-c-06-s04）。
- ox-2b897660b3422458：accepted；s05: 'to frighten you again' is a transitive verb with Jack as experiencer; Anna wants not to cause fear, matching 使害怕.（p05-c-06-s05）。

## 分工煮汤并保存多余份量 · p05-c-07

主题：饮食沟通；情景：分工煮汤并安排剩余份量（soup-cooking-and-portioning）。

沟通目标：确定汤的配料、调味与口感，分配烹饪工作并约定保存多余份量

背景：安迪和贝丝查看食谱，准备今晚的豌豆汤。

人物：Beth、Andy。

起草：author-phase05-c；状态：审阅接受。

- p05-c-07-s01 · Beth: This recipe serves six, but there are only three of us tonight.
- p05-c-07-s02 · Andy: We can freeze the extra portions for another evening.
- p05-c-07-s03 · Beth: Good. The vegetables are mainly peas, with one onion and a potato.
- p05-c-07-s04 · Andy: We have frozen peas. Do I add them at the beginning?
- p05-c-07-s05 · Beth: Not yet. First, I will fry the onion while you cut the potato.
- p05-c-07-s06 · Andy: How much liquid does the recipe call for?
- p05-c-07-s07 · Beth: A litre of stock. Then we cook the vegetables until they are soft.
- p05-c-07-s08 · Andy: Would you like me to mix in the cream at the end?
- p05-c-07-s09 · Beth: Yes, but only half of it. I want a thick mixture, not a thin soup.
- p05-c-07-s10 · Andy: What about this chilli powder?
- p05-c-07-s11 · Beth: Let us keep the soup mild. You can add some to your own bowl.
- p05-c-07-s12 · Andy: Done. I will start on the potato and set out three bowls and containers for the rest.

目标用法：

- freeze · v. · 冷冻保存食物；义项 sense-staged-ox-77f16496a1ce4f2e-p05-c-07；来源 ox-77f16496a1ce4f2e；证据 p05-c-07-s02；词形 freeze。

- fry · v. · 油煎；油炒；义项 sense-staged-ox-7e180134049e5042-p05-c-07；来源 ox-7e180134049e5042；证据 p05-c-07-s05；词形 fry。
- liquid · n. · 液体；义项 sense-staged-ox-edfcf7a058099740-p05-c-07；来源 ox-edfcf7a058099740；证据 p05-c-07-s06；词形 liquid。

- mixture · n. · 混合物；义项 sense-staged-ox-322d19e5f9b45e6f-p05-c-07；来源 ox-322d19e5f9b45e6f；证据 p05-c-07-s09；词形 mixture。

Beth and Andy plan portions, freezing leftovers, preparation duties, stock, cream and individual chilli. The ending begins preparation and sets out containers rather than claiming cooking is done. Title and planning goal fit; litre and chilli are consistent British spellings.

- ox-77f16496a1ce4f2e：accepted；s02: 'freeze the extra portions' is a transitive verb for preserving soup for a later evening; 冷冻保存食物/source v. are explicit.（p05-c-07-s02）。
- ox-5ac46e8384a42e9b：accepted；s03: 'the vegetables are mainly peas' uses an adverb for the predominant ingredient, with onion and potato as smaller components; 主要地 fits.（p05-c-07-s03）。
- ox-c4de7be2b2af9718：accepted；s04: 'frozen peas' is an attributive adjective describing stored condition, not an action verb; it naturally contrasts with freezing the future leftovers.（p05-c-07-s04）。
- ox-7e180134049e5042：accepted；s05: 'will fry the onion' is a transitive cooking verb, naturally preceding soup preparation; 油煎/油炒 and source v. fit.（p05-c-07-s05）。
- ox-edfcf7a058099740：accepted；s06: 'how much liquid' is an uncountable noun phrase; the answer 'a litre of stock' identifies the 液体 sense rather than the adjective.（p05-c-07-s06）。
- ox-4c23e079afe6a22a：accepted；s08: 'mix in the cream' is a transitive particle construction meaning incorporate cream into the soup, naturally selecting 混合/拌入 and source v.（p05-c-07-s08）。
- ox-322d19e5f9b45e6f：accepted；s09: 'a thick mixture' is a noun phrase for combined soup ingredients; the thin-soup contrast motivates the consistency sense 混合物.（p05-c-07-s09）。
- ox-258c31e1ab03b530：accepted；s11: 'keep the soup mild' is an adjective object complement. Excluding communal chilli and allowing individual additions make 不辣的 clear.（p05-c-07-s11）。

## 约好送车检查异响 · p05-c-08

主题：消费与服务；情景：预约检查汽车异响（garage-engine-noise-booking）。

沟通目标：描述驾驶中的异响并确认送检及反馈时间

背景：哈里打电话给修车厂，描述汽车最近的异常。

人物：Harry、Priya。

起草：author-phase05-c；状态：审阅接受。

- p05-c-08-s01 · Harry: Is this Green Street garage? I would like to book a check for my car.
- p05-c-08-s02 · Priya: Yes. What seems to be the issue?
- p05-c-08-s03 · Harry: There is a rattling noise when I accelerate, and it is happening more frequently now.
- p05-c-08-s04 · Priya: Have you noticed any other changes?
- p05-c-08-s05 · Harry: There is a loss of power on hills, and I seem to be using more fuel than usual.
- p05-c-08-s06 · Priya: We will need to inspect it before we can explain the cause. Can you bring it in at nine tomorrow?
- p05-c-08-s07 · Harry: Yes. Will someone call me before doing any repair work?
- p05-c-08-s08 · Priya: Of course. We will call you by noon with what we find and a price before going ahead.
- p05-c-08-s09 · Harry: Thank you. I will bring it in at nine.

- garage · n. · 汽车修理厂；义项 sense-staged-ox-1090094a846bf1e8-p05-c-08；来源 ox-1090094a846bf1e8；证据 p05-c-08-s01；词形 garage。

- fuel · n. · 燃料；义项 sense-staged-ox-6045c41fcbdb2f95-p05-c-08；来源 ox-6045c41fcbdb2f95；证据 p05-c-08-s05；词形 fuel。

Harry reports rattling, power loss and fuel use; Priya waits for inspection before explaining the cause. They agree on a nine-o'clock drop-off and noon callback before repairs. Customer/garage roles, complete booking ending, title and goal match.

## 在工作人员陪同下先见见狗 · p05-c-09

主题：自然与环境；情景：安排初次接触收容所的狗（shelter-dog-first-meeting）。

沟通目标：说明养狗顾虑并同意在工作人员陪同下先短暂见面

背景：米娜第一次到收容所了解领养，向工作人员表示对接触狗有些担心。

人物：Mina、Eli。

起草：author-phase05-c；状态：审阅接受。

- p05-c-09-s01 · Mina: I like the little brown dog, but I have never lived with one before.
- p05-c-09-s02 · Eli: That is Bruno. He is usually very gentle, though we still introduce new people slowly.
- p05-c-09-s03 · Mina: His fur looks soft. Would he mind if I touched him?
- p05-c-09-s04 · Eli: Let us meet him first and see how he responds. You do not need to touch him today.
- p05-c-09-s05 · Mina: Could you stay with me while I meet him?
- p05-c-09-s06 · Eli: Certainly. We can spend five minutes together in the quiet room.
- p05-c-09-s07 · Mina: I would like that. I am not ready to decide about adoption yet.
- p05-c-09-s08 · Eli: There is no need to decide today. We will just get to know each other.

- gentle · adj. · 温顺的；温和的；义项 sense-staged-ox-62dff896f9732fa6-p05-c-09；来源 ox-62dff896f9732fa6；证据 p05-c-09-s02；词形 gentle。

审阅者：reviewer-phase05-c；整篇结论：accepted。

Mina asks about Bruno and her own uncertainty; Eli proposes a slow, accompanied five-minute meeting without pressure to touch or adopt. The agreed introduction matches the restrained title and goal, with consistent visitor/staff identities.

- ox-e1010c0c8fc7e555：accepted；s03: 'his fur looks soft' uses the mass noun for the living dog's coat. The proposed touch anchors 动物的毛皮 rather than a manufactured fur item.（p05-c-09-s03）。

## 为进口货物延误调整备货 · p05-c-10

主题：工作与协作；情景：商定进口货物延误的应对安排（imported-stock-delay-plan）。

沟通目标：核实延误对店铺的影响，并决定询问本地替代品报价及更新预计到货通知

背景：一家小店的采购员和店长讨论供应商发来的延误通知；情节虚构。

人物：Asha、Ben。

起草：author-phase05-c；状态：审阅接受。

- p05-c-10-s01 · Asha: The supplier says our goods will arrive two weeks late.
- p05-c-10-s02 · Ben: Is that just our shipment, or is there a wider problem?
- p05-c-10-s03 · Asha: They describe it as a global shipping delay, but we only know the date for our own order.
- p05-c-10-s04 · Ben: We import all our large baskets from them. What is the impact on next week's display?
- p05-c-10-s05 · Asha: We have enough small baskets, but only two large ones left.
- p05-c-10-s06 · Ben: Then let us ask the local maker for a small batch rather than promise stock we do not have.
- p05-c-10-s07 · Asha: I agree. This shows the importance of having a second supplier.
- p05-c-10-s08 · Ben: Please get a local quote today. I will update the display notice with the new expected date.

- goods · n. · 商品；货物；义项 sense-staged-ox-7afd3ac6f01020b1-p05-c-10；来源 ox-7afd3ac6f01020b1；证据 p05-c-10-s01；词形 goods。
- global · adj. · 全球性的；义项 sense-staged-ox-e834aaba5adc0f3f-p05-c-10；来源 ox-e834aaba5adc0f3f；证据 p05-c-10-s03；词形 global。

- impact · n. · 影响；义项 sense-staged-ox-0c6deeea99d4aecc-p05-c-10；来源 ox-0c6deeea99d4aecc；证据 p05-c-10-s04；词形 impact。

审阅者：reviewer-phase05-c；整篇结论：accepted。

Asha and Ben distinguish the supplier's broad claim from their known delay, assess basket stock, and assign a local quote and notice update. The fictional shop setting, stable roles and concrete planning ending support the contingency title/goal without claiming a purchase.

- ox-18c4ab0bfd02eb48：accepted；s04: 'we import all our large baskets' is a finite transitive verb for buying in from abroad; the local-maker contrast clarifies 进口, not import n.（p05-c-10-s04）。
- ox-0c6deeea99d4aecc：accepted；s04: 'the impact on next week's display' is a noun phrase for an effect, with the standard on complement; the stock assessment grounds 影响.（p05-c-10-s04）。

## 找到数学作业的失分步骤 · p05-c-11

主题：学习与技能；情景：根据数学作业反馈安排练习（math-feedback-study-plan）。

沟通目标：找出计算失误的位置并商定重做与检查的具体步骤

背景：学生芬恩拿着批改后的数学作业向老师请教。

人物：Finn、Ms Patel。

起草：author-phase05-c；状态：审阅接受。

- p05-c-11-s01 · Finn: My grade has gone up, but I still lost marks on the last two questions.
- p05-c-11-s02 · Ms Patel: There is a real improvement in how you set out your work. Let us look at where the calculation changes.
- p05-c-11-s03 · Finn: I can hardly read the number I wrote on this line. Is that a three or an eight?
- p05-c-11-s04 · Ms Patel: You copied the three as an eight in the next step. The method itself is right.
- p05-c-11-s05 · Finn: I am keen to stop making mistakes like that. Should I do twenty more questions?
- p05-c-11-s06 · Ms Patel: Start by doing these two again, with one step on each line. Then check each copied number against the question.
- p05-c-11-s07 · Finn: I will redo them now and circle the numbers while I check.
- p05-c-11-s08 · Ms Patel: Good. Bring me the two new solutions when you finish, and we will compare them.

- grade · n. · 成绩等级；分数；义项 sense-staged-ox-79d2a82b848e75d6-p05-c-11；来源 ox-79d2a82b848e75d6；证据 p05-c-11-s01；词形 grade。
- improvement · n. · 改进；进步；义项 sense-staged-ox-ae74b0d44c4cac98-p05-c-11；来源 ox-ae74b0d44c4cac98；证据 p05-c-11-s02；词形 improvement。
- hardly · adv. · 几乎不；义项 sense-staged-ox-e7b44e2b0db16c8b-p05-c-11；来源 ox-e7b44e2b0db16c8b；证据 p05-c-11-s03；词形 hardly。
- keen · adj. · 热切的；渴望的；义项 sense-staged-ox-5001d5500e5ec35a-p05-c-11；来源 ox-5001d5500e5ec35a；证据 p05-c-11-s05；词形 keen。

审阅者：reviewer-phase05-c；整篇结论：accepted。

Finn and Ms Patel identify a copied-digit error, keep the valid method, and agree on two redone questions and number checks rather than indiscriminate practice. The comparison offer closes a coherent teacher/student exchange matching title and goal.

- ox-ae74b0d44c4cac98：accepted；s02: 'a real improvement in how you set out your work' is a noun for progress, with an in complement identifying the improved skill.（p05-c-11-s02）。

## 为营销毕业生确定求职第一步 · p05-c-12

主题：工作与协作；情景：安排营销毕业生的求职准备（marketing-graduate-job-search）。

沟通目标：根据经验和收入需求筛选入门岗位，并确定准备作品和咨询职位的下一步

起草：author-phase05-c；状态：审阅接受。

- p05-c-12-s03 · Luca: Marketing. I enjoyed planning campaigns, but I have no experience in management.
- p05-c-12-s04 · Bella: Then look at assistant roles rather than positions where you would lead a team.
- p05-c-12-s05 · Luca: Some of the internships are unpaid. I need to earn a living, so those are not realistic for me.
- p05-c-12-s06 · Bella: Let us filter for paid entry-level work. Do you have a project you could show an employer?
- p05-c-12-s07 · Luca: I created a campaign for our student theatre. I could put the posters and results together.
- p05-c-12-s08 · Bella: Do that first. Then choose two suitable listings, and we can check the requirements together on Friday.
- p05-c-12-s09 · Luca: Thanks. I will bring the project and two listings to our Friday appointment.

## 用具体成果练习面试回答 · p05-c-13

主题：工作与协作；情景：准备求职面试例子（job-interview-example）。

沟通目标：把笼统自我评价改成具体工作例子

背景：米娅请朋友雷练习一段求职面试回答。

人物：Mia、Ray。

起草：author-phase05-c；状态：审阅接受。

- p05-c-13-s01 · Mia: I have an interview with a leading design agency, and I want to make a good impression.
- p05-c-13-s02 · Ray: How are you planning to describe your work?
- p05-c-13-s03 · Mia: I was going to say that my last project was impressive and that everyone loved it.
- p05-c-13-s04 · Ray: That tells them your opinion, but not what you did. What was the problem you solved?
- p05-c-13-s05 · Mia: The old booking form was incredibly confusing. Customers kept calling because they could not find the date selector.
- p05-c-13-s06 · Ray: What did you change?
- p05-c-13-s07 · Mia: I moved it to the first page and tested the form with five customers. All five completed it without help.
- p05-c-13-s08 · Ray: That is much clearer. You do not need to call yourself highly talented if you can explain your work.
- p05-c-13-s09 · Mia: Right. I will start with the problem, describe my change, and give the test result.
- p05-c-13-s10 · Ray: Try that answer once more, and I will listen without interrupting.

- leading · adj. · 领先的；重要的；义项 sense-staged-ox-e171c4dc457e9995-p05-c-13；来源 ox-e171c4dc457e9995；证据 p05-c-13-s01；词形 leading。

审阅者：reviewer-phase05-c；整篇结论：accepted。

Ray helps Mia replace unsupported praise with a concrete booking-form problem, design change and five-customer test. Mia states the revised answer structure and Ray invites another rehearsal. This natural practice ending matches the title and goal without pretending the repeat has occurred.

- ox-aae30582d0854660：accepted；s01: 'make a good impression' is the natural verb-noun collocation for how the interviewer perceives Mia, precisely expressing 印象.（p05-c-13-s01）。

## 只改变水量的种子实验 · p05-c-14

主题：学习与技能；情景：制定种子生长对照实验（seed-trial-design）。

沟通目标：只改变浇水量并约定观察记录方式

背景：两名学生在教师指定的实验室里计划小麦种子的生长观察。

人物：Tara、Niko。

起草：author-phase05-c；状态：审阅接受。

- p05-c-14-s01 · Tara: We have two identical pots ready in the laboratory. What will we change between them?
- p05-c-14-s02 · Niko: Only the amount of water. Each pot will get one grain of wheat from the same packet.
- p05-c-14-s03 · Tara: And the soil must be the same in both. Shall we cover each seed with a thin layer of it?
- p05-c-14-s04 · Niko: Yes, the same depth in each pot. We will keep them beside the same window.
- p05-c-14-s05 · Tara: How will we record growth?
- p05-c-14-s06 · Niko: Measure the height every morning and write it in our journal, along with the amount of water.
- p05-c-14-s07 · Tara: Should we also record when the first leaf appears?
- p05-c-14-s08 · Niko: Definitely. But one taller plant would not indicate that our result applies to all wheat.
- p05-c-14-s09 · Tara: Then we will call this a small trial and include that limit in the report.
- p05-c-14-s10 · Niko: Agreed. I will label the pots, and you can prepare the record sheet before we plant.

- laboratory · n. · 实验室；义项 sense-staged-ox-3b666f8ea839ff1c-p05-c-14；来源 ox-3b666f8ea839ff1c；证据 p05-c-14-s01；词形 laboratory。

- growth · n. · 生长；义项 sense-staged-ox-be8e69ac1c4220fe-p05-c-14；来源 ox-be8e69ac1c4220fe；证据 p05-c-14-s05；词形 growth。

Tara and Niko hold pots, seed source, soil depth and location constant, vary water, and agree to record height and first leaf. They acknowledge the small trial's limits and assign setup duties. The title/goal and lab context match planning rather than completed results.

## 婚礼致辞只讲双方愿意分享的事 · p05-c-16

主题：家庭与人际；情景：确认婚礼致辞内容边界（wedding-toast-boundaries）。

沟通目标：确定致辞中的感谢和祝愿，并同意删去私密故事及不切实际的承诺

背景：新郎奥斯卡和姐姐妮娜讨论她准备在婚礼上说的话。

人物：Nina、Oscar。

起草：author-phase05-c；状态：审阅接受。

- p05-c-16-s01 · Nina: I have written a short toast for your wedding. Is there anything you would rather I left out?
- p05-c-16-s02 · Oscar: Please skip the story about our first kiss. Ana would prefer to keep that private.
- p05-c-16-s03 · Nina: Of course. I mainly want to say how much happiness you bring each other.
- p05-c-16-s04 · Oscar: That is lovely. Could you also thank Aunt Rosa for being so generous with her time? She made all the decorations.
- p05-c-16-s05 · Nina: I will. Are you comfortable with me talking about what marriage means to you?
- p05-c-16-s06 · Oscar: Yes, but please do not say we will agree on everything forever. We certainly do not agree on everything now.
- p05-c-16-s07 · Nina: Fair point. I will wish you patience and plenty of laughter instead.
- p05-c-16-s08 · Oscar: I am grateful you are doing this. Keep it under two minutes, and it will be perfect.
- p05-c-16-s09 · Nina: Two minutes, a thank-you to Rosa, and no private stories. I will make those changes tonight.

## 去掉未证实的救援说法，改用天气标题 · p05-c-17

主题：社会与公共事务；情景：核实风暴报道与标题（storm-news-verification）。

沟通目标：区分已确认的天气信息和未证实的救援消息，并约定修改报道及继续核实

背景：新闻写作课中，两名学生核对一则完全虚构的风暴报道练习。

人物：Clara、Dev。

起草：author-phase05-c；状态：审阅接受。

- p05-c-17-s01 · Clara: Your headline says twenty people were rescued from the island. Where did that number come from?
- p05-c-17-s02 · Dev: A post from a local resident. There is a photograph of a helicopter above the harbour.
- p05-c-17-s03 · Clara: The photograph does not show how many people were on board, or why it was there.
- p05-c-17-s04 · Dev: You are right. The latest weather bulletin does confirm that a hurricane passed the island overnight.
- p05-c-17-s05 · Clara: And our reporter says it is still raining heavily. Those details have a clear source.
- p05-c-17-s06 · Dev: I will remove the rescue number and ask the emergency office for confirmation.
- p05-c-17-s07 · Clara: Meanwhile, we can finish the section about the weather. Let us use a title that does not claim a rescue.
- p05-c-17-s08 · Dev: How about: Hurricane passes island as heavy rain continues?
- p05-c-17-s09 · Clara: That matches what we know. Use that, and leave the rescue paragraph out until we have an answer.

- latest · adj. · 最新的；义项 sense-staged-ox-77bcb42e22b41f16-p05-c-17；来源 ox-77bcb42e22b41f16；证据 p05-c-17-s04；词形 latest。
- hurricane · n. · 飓风；义项 sense-staged-ox-ae8ecb6cff2bf031-p05-c-17；来源 ox-ae8ecb6cff2bf031；证据 p05-c-17-s04；词形 hurricane。

Reread the complete version-2 dialogue and all six targets. The new title accurately describes removing the unverified rescue claim and using a weather headline. Clara challenges the unsupported number; Dev agrees to seek confirmation; meanwhile they select a headline limited to sourced hurricane/rain information and leave the rescue paragraph out. This natural final decision matches the scenario goal and fictional classroom context, with stable speaker roles and British harbour. The earlier title/outcome sequencing defect is resolved.
