## 让家族展览说明有材料可查 · p05-c-19

主题：学习与技能；情景：修订家族移民经历展览说明（family-migration-exhibit-text）。

沟通目标：核对人物经历与历史材料，限定有证据支持的影响描述并安排补充出处

背景：学生萝丝和展览指导老师准备一份虚构的家族历史展示。

人物：Rose、Mr Chen。

起草：author-phase05-c；状态：审阅接受。

- p05-c-19-s01 · Rose: My grandmother was an immigrant. She arrived here at nineteen and later worked for the town council.
- p05-c-19-s02 · Mr Chen: What evidence do you have for the dates in your display?
- p05-c-19-s03 · Rose: These historical documents include her arrival record and a letter offering her the council job.
- p05-c-19-s04 · Mr Chen: Your next caption says she changed the whole town. Can you show how?
- p05-c-19-s05 · Rose: She helped with the first election in which women here could vote. The museum describes it as a historic election.
- p05-c-19-s06 · Mr Chen: That establishes why the election mattered, but not how much your grandmother shaped it.
- p05-c-19-s07 · Rose: Then I should not say she had a major influence on the result. The letter only says she helped people register.
- p05-c-19-s08 · Mr Chen: Exactly. Describe that work and name the source beside the caption.
- p05-c-19-s09 · Rose: I will replace the broad claim with her documented role and add references to the letter and museum record.

Rose and Mr Chen distinguish the election's significance from her grandmother's documented role, narrow the claim to registration help, and agree on source references. The fictional research exchange has a clear revision ending matching the title/goal and teacher/student roles.

## 核对悬疑剧角色再继续排练 · p05-c-23

主题：文娱与运动；情景：排练悬疑剧片段（mystery-scene-rehearsal）。

沟通目标：澄清虚构案件的角色关系并确定继续排练的位置。

背景：导演和演员排练一部虚构的法庭悬疑剧，讨论剧情而非真实案件。

人物：Hugo、Lena。

起草：author-phase05-c；状态：审阅接受。

- p05-c-23-s01 · Hugo: Before we start, why does my character investigate the murder herself? Is she a detective?
- p05-c-23-s02 · Lena: Yes. I play the judge, and you are the detective called to explain the evidence.
- p05-c-23-s03 · Hugo: Does the audience see the killing?
- p05-c-23-s04 · Lena: No, it happens before the play begins. They hear about it through the witnesses.
- p05-c-23-s05 · Hugo: And I already know that Peter is innocent?
- p05-c-23-s06 · Lena: You believe he is, but you have not found the missing letter that proves where he was.
- p05-c-23-s07 · Hugo: So I should sound certain about Peter, but not explain the whole mystery yet.
- p05-c-23-s08 · Lena: Exactly. Let us begin again at the point where the judge asks you about the letter.

## 恢复编辑器的正常输入方式 · p05-c-24

主题：消费与服务；情景：排查键盘导致的覆盖输入（keyboard-shortcut-troubleshooting）。

沟通目标：确认误触按键造成覆盖输入，关闭该功能并实际验证文字恢复正常

背景：员工多拉通过内部支持电话请本帮助检查一个编辑器的输入问题。

人物：Dora、Ben。

- p05-c-24-s01 · Dora: Is this IT? Whenever I type, the next letter disappears.
- p05-c-24-s02 · Ben: Yes, I can help. Which keys did you press just before it started?
- p05-c-24-s03 · Dora: I dropped a notebook on the keyboard. Now I have made a mess of this sentence.
- p05-c-24-s04 · Ben: I can see the screen you shared. This editor is in overwrite mode.
- p05-c-24-s05 · Dora: What is the function of the key labelled Insert? I think the notebook hit that one.
- p05-c-24-s06 · Ben: In this editor, it switches overwrite mode on and off. Press it once, then undo the damaged sentence.
- p05-c-24-s07 · Dora: The sentence is back. I will try adding a word in the middle.
- p05-c-24-s08 · Ben: Does the following text stay there now?
- p05-c-24-s09 · Dora: Yes, it moves along instead of disappearing. That has fixed it. Thank you.

审阅者：reviewer-phase05-c；整篇结论：accepted。

Dora describes text replacement, Ben identifies overwrite mode from the shared screen and limits the key instruction to this editor, then Dora tests insertion and confirms recovery. The verified ending, support roles and title/goal align; labelled is consistent British spelling.

## 先试办一次小型维修工作坊 · p05-c-25

主题：工作与协作；情景：商定工作坊试办资源（workshop-pilot-resource-agreement）。

沟通目标：根据时间、人员和场地限制缩小试办范围，并明确预算上限和筹备分工

背景：两名志愿者准备在社区中心试办一次物品维修工作坊。

人物：Grace、Omar。

起草：author-phase05-c；状态：审阅接受。

- p05-c-25-s01 · Grace: Do you intend to run this every weekend? I cannot give up that much time.
- p05-c-25-s02 · Omar: No. My intention is to try one Saturday session and see whether people find it useful.
- p05-c-25-s03 · Grace: In that case, I would like to stay involved. I can help with the bookings.
- p05-c-25-s04 · Omar: Thanks. How much time could you invest in preparing the first session?
- p05-c-25-s05 · Grace: Two hours this week. Our bigger problem is the lack of storage space for equipment.
- p05-c-25-s06 · Omar: Then let us borrow tools on the day rather than buy anything we have to keep here.
- p05-c-25-s07 · Grace: That also helps with the budget. We have a limit of fifty pounds for this trial.
- p05-c-25-s08 · Omar: Agreed. I will ask the tool library about borrowing, and you can prepare a booking list for ten places.
- p05-c-25-s09 · Grace: I will do that tonight. We can check the arrangements together before we advertise.

- intend · v. · 打算；义项 sense-staged-ox-caded308b36964ed-p05-c-25；来源 ox-caded308b36964ed；证据 p05-c-25-s01；词形 intend。
- intention · n. · 意图；打算；义项 sense-staged-ox-89c4daeaa1516174-p05-c-25；来源 ox-89c4daeaa1516174；证据 p05-c-25-s02；词形 intention。
- involved · adj. · 参与其中的；义项 sense-staged-ox-bc05e5d6b770501b-p05-c-25；来源 ox-bc05e5d6b770501b；证据 p05-c-25-s03；词形 involved。
- invest · v. · 投入时间或精力；义项 sense-staged-ox-227f9055367545d4-p05-c-25；来源 ox-227f9055367545d4；证据 p05-c-25-s04；词形 invest。
- lack · n. · 缺乏；义项 sense-staged-ox-28741b783c366c40-p05-c-25；来源 ox-28741b783c366c40；证据 p05-c-25-s05；词形 lack。

审阅者：reviewer-phase05-c；整篇结论：accepted。

Grace's time concern narrows Omar's plan to one trial. They address storage, borrowing, budget and ten places, and divide preparations before advertising. The bounded plan and explicit duties match the title/goal with consistent volunteer roles.

- ox-89c4daeaa1516174：accepted；s02: 'my intention is to try one Saturday session' uses a noun subject naming the intended plan, naturally contrasting with the preceding intend verb.（p05-c-25-s02）。

- ox-227f9055367545d4：accepted；s04: 'how much time could you invest in preparing' uses a verb taking the questioned time as object. The two-hour reply makes 时间/精力投入 explicit, not finance.（p05-c-25-s04）。

## 选一本适合共读的温暖故事 · p05-c-26

主题：文娱与运动；情景：选择读书小组作品（reading-group-book-choice）。

沟通目标：比较故事题材与讨论安排后选定共读作品

背景：刚搬来的索拉向图书管理员询问读书小组，希望找到轻松阅读和认识邻居的机会。

人物：Sora、Ned。

- p05-c-26-s01 · Sora: I love literature, but I have not joined a reading group before.
- p05-c-26-s02 · Ned: What do you like to read in your leisure time?
- p05-c-26-s03 · Sora: Usually crime stories, though I am not in the mood for anything dark at the moment.
- p05-c-26-s04 · Ned: Then you might prefer The Harbour Kitchen to our other choice, Winter Trial. One is about neighbours opening a cafe; the other is a courtroom drama.
- p05-c-26-s05 · Sora: The cafe story sounds warmer. I have felt a little lonely since I moved here.
- p05-c-26-s06 · Ned: Our group is friendly, and you can listen at first if you do not feel like speaking.
- p05-c-26-s07 · Sora: When would we discuss The Harbour Kitchen?
- p05-c-26-s08 · Ned: Next Thursday at seven. We are only reading the first four chapters for that meeting.
- p05-c-26-s09 · Sora: That feels manageable. I will borrow it today and join you on Thursday.
- p05-c-26-s10 · Ned: Lovely. We meet at the round table upstairs.

审阅者：reviewer-phase05-c；整篇结论：accepted。

Ned compares book tone with Sora's preferences, explains low-pressure participation and supplies reading amount, date and place. Sora chooses the cafe story and agrees to attend. The selected-work ending, librarian/newcomer roles and title/goal are coherent.

- ox-386b06dbd6af77fc：accepted；s03: 'not in the mood for anything dark' uses the noun idiom for present emotional inclination; it naturally links book tone to 心情/情绪.（p05-c-26-s03）。

## 比较材质后选一只日常用包 · p05-c-27

- p05-c-27-s01 · Nia: Is this brown bag made of leather?

- p05-c-27-s03 · Nia: The brown one is beautiful, but at that price it would be a luxury for me.
- p05-c-27-s04 · Seth: What will you carry in it most days?
- p05-c-27-s05 · Nia: A notebook and my lunch. A medium bag should be enough; the large one looks awkward.
- p05-c-27-s06 · Seth: Try your notebook in the blue one. The label shows both the size and the price.
- p05-c-27-s07 · Nia: It fits, and the straps feel comfortable. I will take this one for thirty pounds.
- p05-c-27-s08 · Seth: Certainly. I will remove the display tag and ring it up for you.

## 看清足迹但不跟进草丛 · p05-c-28

主题：自然与环境；情景：观察野生动物足迹（wildlife-track-observation）。

- p05-c-28-s01 · Jade: There are little prints in the mud beside the path. Could they be from a fox?
- p05-c-28-s02 · Ranger Theo: Possibly, but the edges are blurred. I would not identify the animal from these alone.
- p05-c-28-s03 · Jade: Do foxes hunt in this field?
- p05-c-28-s04 · Ranger Theo: Sometimes. We have seen them searching for small animals here in the evening.
- p05-c-28-s05 · Jade: Shall we follow the prints into the grass?
- p05-c-28-s06 · Ranger Theo: Let us stay on the path. We can look through the binoculars without disturbing whatever is there.
- p05-c-28-s07 · Jade: Good idea. I will take a photograph of the prints from here first.

审阅者：reviewer-phase05-c；整篇结论：accepted。

Jade asks about blurred prints and fox activity; Theo avoids firm identification and recommends viewing from the path. Jade agrees to photograph the prints from there. The ending matches the title's staying-out-of-grass emphasis and observation goal.

## 核对开车去乡间博物馆的路线 · p05-c-29

主题：出行与住宿；情景：核对租车出游的博物馆路线（rental-car-museum-route）。

沟通目标：确认自驾需求、博物馆位置与正确入口，并在比较路线后选定行车路线

背景：游客拉维在游客中心询问第二天租车前往乡间博物馆的路线。

人物：Ravi、Nell。

起草：author-phase05-c；状态：审阅接受。

- p05-c-29-s01 · Ravi: We plan to hire a car tomorrow and visit the rural museum. Can you show me the way?
- p05-c-29-s02 · Nell: Certainly. The museum is located east of the river, just beyond Ashford.
- p05-c-29-s03 · Ravi: My phone gives me two roads. Is the highway the better choice?
- p05-c-29-s04 · Nell: It is quicker to Ashford, but you must leave it before the bridge and take the village road.
- p05-c-29-s05 · Ravi: That seems like an indirect route. Why can we not turn off at the museum?
- p05-c-29-s06 · Nell: That entrance is for deliveries. Visitors enter from Mill Lane, on the other side of the building.
- p05-c-29-s07 · Ravi: Can you help me locate that entrance on this map?
- p05-c-29-s08 · Nell: Here, beside the mill. This is the location to put into your navigation app, rather than the delivery gate.
- p05-c-29-s09 · Ravi: I see it now. We will take the highway to Ashford, then follow the village road to Mill Lane. Thank you.

## 核对课堂案例中的转载许可 · p05-c-30

主题：社会与公共事务；情景：核对诗歌转载案例中的许可（poem-reprint-permission-check）。

沟通目标：依据课堂案例给出的许可条件判断所述转载并修正一概而论的说法

背景：学生在课堂上讨论教师编写的虚构版权案例，不涉及真实转载或法律建议。

人物：Imogen、Felix。

起草：author-phase05-c；状态：审阅接受。

- p05-c-30-s01 · Imogen: My answer says it is illegal for the school magazine to print the poem.
- p05-c-30-s02 · Felix: Did you read the last paragraph of the case? The author gave written permission for this issue.
- p05-c-30-s03 · Imogen: I missed that. I assumed every reprint needed a payment.
- p05-c-30-s04 · Felix: In this example, the permission is free, as long as the magazine prints her name and does not change the poem.
- p05-c-30-s05 · Imogen: So the reprint described here is legal because it meets those conditions. I should not give the same answer for every possible case.
- p05-c-30-s06 · Felix: Exactly. Add the conditions to your explanation instead of just changing yes to no.
- p05-c-30-s07 · Imogen: I will. Then I will ask the teacher to check that I have explained the distinction clearly.

目标用法：

- illegal · adj. · 违法的；义项 sense-staged-ox-7f27e994b4fea69c-p05-c-30；来源 ox-7f27e994b4fea69c；证据 p05-c-30-s01；词形 illegal。
- legal · adj. · 合法的；义项 sense-staged-ox-f0866181618ea6ff-p05-c-30；来源 ox-f0866181618ea6ff；证据 p05-c-30-s05；词形 legal。

审阅者：reviewer-phase05-c；整篇结论：accepted。

Imogen corrects a blanket answer after Felix points out the fictional case's permission and conditions. She will explain those conditions and seek teacher feedback. The limited classroom purpose, roles and correction ending match title/goal without advising on a real dispute.

绑定文件 SHA-256：de13581d5f37d0b078b7f660119b7d0f6346b035fcdb39d349a62383c959b749

- ox-7f27e994b4fea69c：accepted；s01: 'it is illegal for the school magazine to print the poem' uses a predicative adjective for the initial classification. The later correction changes the judgement, not the 违法的 meaning.（p05-c-30-s01）。
- ox-f0866181618ea6ff：accepted；s05: 'the reprint described here is legal' uses a predicative adjective for the permission-based corrected judgement; stated case conditions bound the 合法的 claim.（p05-c-30-s05）。

## 凭补缝痕迹认领手套 · p05-c-31

主题：消费与服务；情景：描述并认领遗失手套（lost-glove-description）。

沟通目标：凭颜色和独特修补痕迹确认手套归属并领取

背景：莫在泳池前台询问当天遗失的一只手套。

人物：Mo、Fran。

起草：author-phase05-c；状态：审阅接受。

- p05-c-31-s01 · Mo: Has anyone handed in a grey glove this afternoon? I seem to have lost my right one.
- p05-c-31-s02 · Fran: We have two here. Can you describe anything unusual about yours?
- p05-c-31-s03 · Mo: There is a small repair near the thumb, sewn with bright green thread.
- p05-c-31-s04 · Fran: This one has that repair. Is the matching one in your bag?
- p05-c-31-s05 · Mo: Yes, here it is. They are the same colour and size.
- p05-c-31-s06 · Fran: That is a match. Here you are.
- p05-c-31-s07 · Mo: Thank you. I will put both of them in my bag this time.

- glove · n. · 手套；义项 sense-staged-ox-a32046999b6341a0-p05-c-31；来源 ox-a32046999b6341a0；证据 p05-c-31-s01；词形 glove。

审阅者：reviewer-phase05-c；整篇结论：accepted。

Mo identifies the right glove's distinctive repair and shows its matching partner. Fran confirms and returns it; Mo acknowledges receipt. This completed verification/recovery sequence matches the repair-trace title and goal with consistent customer/front-desk roles.

绑定文件 SHA-256：95706cc845987e0b7bba531a49c948070af4be5e36a7741b5e68dda20fb9c8d6

- ox-a32046999b6341a0：accepted；s01: 'a grey glove' is a singular count noun for the missing hand covering. The right-hand pair, thumb repair and matching glove substantiate 手套.（p05-c-31-s01）。

<a id="dialogue-p05-c-32"></a>

## 不要把拼图成绩等同于整体智力 · p05-c-32

主题：研究与论证；情景：审视拼图成绩的推论边界（puzzle-intelligence-claim-review）。

沟通目标：区分特定任务表现与整体智力，并依据练习影响修订课堂研究结论

背景：两名学生讨论虚构课堂小实验的报告，准备缩小结论范围。

人物：Bea、Yusuf。

起草：author-phase05-c；状态：审阅接受。

- p05-c-32-s01 · Bea: We finished the puzzle faster on our second attempt. Can we say that the exercise increased our intelligence?
- p05-c-32-s02 · Yusuf: That seems too broad. It is one mental task, and we already knew how the puzzle worked on the second attempt.
- p05-c-32-s03 · Bea: The group generally finished faster the second time, though two people did not.
- p05-c-32-s04 · Yusuf: That does indeed show a change in performance on this puzzle. It does not tell us what happened to every other ability.
- p05-c-32-s05 · Bea: Then we should describe the faster times without claiming an overall change in ability.
- p05-c-32-s06 · Yusuf: Yes, and mention that practice could explain the difference. We did not include a group that was seeing the puzzle for the first time.
- p05-c-32-s07 · Bea: I will rewrite the conclusion around those results and add the missing comparison as a limitation.
- p05-c-32-s08 · Yusuf: Good. I will check that our chart includes the two people whose times did not improve.

目标用法：

- intelligence · n. · 智力；义项 sense-staged-ox-d696bf53632970e8-p05-c-32；来源 ox-d696bf53632970e8；证据 p05-c-32-s01；词形 intelligence。
- mental · adj. · 思维的；脑力的；义项 sense-staged-ox-4fdedcc475773f22-p05-c-32；来源 ox-4fdedcc475773f22；证据 p05-c-32-s02；词形 mental。
- generally · adv. · 总体上；大体上；义项 sense-staged-ox-d5eea74662d0195f-p05-c-32；来源 ox-d5eea74662d0195f；证据 p05-c-32-s03；词形 generally。
- indeed · adv. · 确实；的确；义项 sense-staged-ox-f102a5d9287d9612-p05-c-32；来源 ox-f102a5d9287d9612；证据 p05-c-32-s04；词形 indeed。

审阅者：reviewer-phase05-c；整篇结论：accepted。

Bea and Yusuf distinguish faster puzzle performance from general intelligence, note practice effects and non-improvers, and divide conclusion/chart revisions. The fictional classroom argument has a clear, limited conclusion and ending matching the title/goal.

绑定文件 SHA-256：1e63471f173626880a500d594b4699b2f270d0b590cdaea0bd8fb5f105c3b33b

- ox-d696bf53632970e8：accepted；s01: 'increased our intelligence' uses an abstract noun for general cognitive ability; contrasting it with one-puzzle performance makes 智力 unambiguous.（p05-c-32-s01）。
- ox-4fdedcc475773f22：accepted；s02: 'one mental task' uses an attributive adjective for cognitive work; the puzzle context supports 思维的/脑力的 rather than mental-health status.（p05-c-32-s02）。
- ox-d5eea74662d0195f：accepted；s03: 'the group generally finished faster' uses an adverb for an overall tendency allowing two exceptions, directly illustrating 总体上/大体上.（p05-c-32-s03）。
- ox-f102a5d9287d9612：accepted；s04: 'does indeed show a change' uses an emphatic adverb between auxiliary and verb. Yusuf affirms the narrow finding before rejecting the broad inference, matching 确实/的确.（p05-c-32-s04）。
